Create `OrderDetailView.java`：

```java
package com.lumen.order.dto;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * 委托单详情 (Iteration 4.6).
 *
 * <p>{@code allowedNextStates} = {@code StateMachineEngine.allowedNext("ORDER", 当前态)},
 * 前端据此渲染可执行动作按钮 —— 这是本轮唯一一处「表驱动状态机反向驱动 UI」的地方。
 */
public record OrderDetailView(
        Long id,
        String orderNo,
        Long customerId,
        String businessType,
        String tradeDirection,
        String incoterms,
        String serviceType,
        String originAddress,
        String destAddress,
        BigDecimal totalQty,
        BigDecimal totalWeightKg,
        BigDecimal totalVolumeCbm,
        String orderStatus,
        String settlementCurrency,
        LocalDate etd,
        LocalDate eta,
        LocalDateTime createdAt,
        LocalDate cargoReadyDate,
        Integer containerQty,
        String containerTypeCode,
        String marksAndNos,
        String cargoDescription,
        Integer isDangerous,
        Integer isOversized,
        String temperatureRange,
        String remark,
        String cancelReason,
        String closeReason,
        String voidReason,
        LocalDateTime submittedAt,
        LocalDateTime confirmedAt,
        LocalDateTime deliveredAt,
        LocalDateTime settledAt,
        LocalDateTime closedAt,
        Long createdBy,
        Long updatedBy,
        List<CargoView> cargo,
        List<ServiceItemView> serviceItems,
        List<String> allowedNextStates
) {}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 建 `OrderService` 接口**

Create `lumen-parent/lumen-order/src/main/java/com/lumen/order/service/OrderService.java`：

```java
package com.lumen.order.service;

import com.lumen.common.api.PageResult;
import com.lumen.order.dto.CreateOrderRequest;
import com.lumen.order.dto.OrderDetailView;
import com.lumen.order.dto.OrderView;

import java.util.List;

/**
 * 货运委托单业务接口 (Iteration 4.6).
 *
 * <p><b>门户端也用这个接口</b> —— {@code com.lumen.customer.controller.PortalFreightOrderController}
 * 调下面带 {@code customerId} 的那组方法拿客户归属边界, 门户控制器不做任何状态机判定。
 */
public interface OrderService {

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ 管理端

    /** 建草稿。委托单号由 {@code NumberGenerator.generate("ORDER")} 生成。 */
    OrderView create(CreateOrderRequest request);

    /** 编辑 (仅 DRAFT / PENDING_CONFIRM 可编辑, 否则抛 ORDER_NOT_EDITABLE)。 */
    OrderDetailView update(Long id, CreateOrderRequest request);

    /** 分页列表。 */
    PageResult<OrderView> page(long current, long size, String status, Long customerId,
                                String orderNo, String businessType);

    /** 详情。 */
    OrderDetailView get(Long id);

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ 门户端 (客户归属边界)

    /** 列当前客户的委托单。 */
    PageResult<OrderView> pageByCustomer(Long customerId, long current, long size, String status);

    /** 取详情。**归属不符 → ORDER_NOT_FOUND (404)**, 不是 403 —— 不能泄露「存在但不属于你」。 */
    OrderDetailView getOwned(Long id, Long customerId);

    /** 客户建单 (强制 DRAFT 态)。 */
    OrderView createForCustomer(Long customerId, CreateOrderRequest request);

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ 状态转换 (Task 4)

    OrderDetailView submit(Long id);
    OrderDetailView confirm(Long id);
    OrderDetailView returnToDraft(Long id, String reason);
    OrderDetailView cancel(Long id, String reason);
    OrderDetailView voidOrder(Long id, String reason);
    OrderDetailView startOperation(Long id);
    OrderDetailView deliver(Long id);
    OrderDetailView settle(Long id);
    OrderDetailView close(Long id, String reason);

    /** 门户端确认 (归属校验 + 状态机)。 */
    OrderDetailView confirmOwned(Long id, Long customerId);

    /** 门户端取消 (归属校验 + 状态机)。 */
    OrderDetailView cancelOwned(Long id, Long customerId, String reason);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: 建 `OrderServiceImpl` (创建 / 编辑 / 查询部分)**

Create `lumen-parent/lumen-order/src/main/java/com/lumen/order/service/impl/OrderServiceImpl.java`：

```java
package com.lumen.order.service.impl;

import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.core.conditions.query.LambdaQueryWrapper;
import com.baomidou.mybatisplus.extension.plugins.pagination.Page;
import com.lumen.common.api.PageResult;
import com.lumen.common.tenant.TenantContext;
import com.lumen.numbering.service.NumberGenerator;
import com.lumen.order.OrderConstants;
import com.lumen.order.dto.CargoRequest;
import com.lumen.order.dto.CargoView;
import com.lumen.order.dto.CreateOrderRequest;
import com.lumen.order.dto.OrderDetailView;
import com.lumen.order.dto.OrderView;
import com.lumen.order.dto.ServiceItemRequest;
import com.lumen.order.dto.ServiceItemView;
import com.lumen.order.entity.Order;
import com.lumen.order.entity.OrderCargo;
import com.lumen.order.entity.OrderServiceItem;
import com.lumen.order.error.OrderErrorCode;
import com.lumen.order.exception.OrderException;
import com.lumen.order.mapper.OrderCargoMapper;
import com.lumen.order.mapper.OrderMapper;
import com.lumen.order.mapper.OrderServiceItemMapper;
import com.lumen.order.service.OrderService;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.stereotype.Service;
import org.springframework.transaction.annotation.Transactional;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.time.LocalDateTime;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * 货运委托单业务实现 (Iteration 4.6).
 *
 * <p><b>租户</b>: {@code tenant_id} 由 {@code MyMetaObjectHandler} 在插入时按
 * {@code TenantContext} 自动填; 查询侧由 {@code MybatisPlusConfig} 的
 * {@code TenantLineInnerInterceptor} 自动加 {@code AND tenant_id = ?}。本类**不手动**设 tenantId。
 *
 * <p><b>软删</b>: 三个实体的 {@code deleted} 都带 {@code @TableLogic}, MP 自动补 {@code deleted = 0}。
 */
@Slf4j
@Service
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class OrderServiceImpl implements OrderService {

    private final OrderMapper orderMapper;
    private final OrderCargoMapper cargoMapper;
    private final OrderServiceItemMapper serviceItemMapper;
    private final NumberGenerator numberGenerator;

    /** 分页 size 上限 —— 与 CsExceptionController 一致。 */
    private static final long MAX_PAGE_SIZE = 200L;

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ 管理端

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderView create(CreateOrderRequest request) {
        return createInternal(request, null, OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderDetailView update(Long id, CreateOrderRequest request) {
        Order order = requireOrder(id);
        if (!OrderConstants.EDITABLE_STATES.contains(order.getOrderStatus())) {
            throw new OrderException(OrderErrorCode.ORDER_NOT_EDITABLE,
                    "当前状态 " + order.getOrderStatus() + " 不可编辑");
        }
        List<CargoRequest> cargo = requireCargo(request);
        List<ServiceItemRequest> items = requireServiceItems(request);

        applyHeader(order, request, null);
        orderMapper.updateById(order);

        replaceChildren(order.getId(), cargo, items);
        return get(id);
    }

    @Override
    public PageResult<OrderView> page(long current, long size, String status, Long customerId,
                                       String orderNo, String businessType) {
        long effectiveSize = Math.min(size <= 0 ? 20 : size, MAX_PAGE_SIZE);
        long pageNum = current <= 0 ? 1 : current;

        LambdaQueryWrapper<Order> q = new LambdaQueryWrapper<Order>()
                .orderByDesc(Order::getCreatedAt);
        if (status != null && !status.isBlank()) {
            q.eq(Order::getOrderStatus, status);
        }
        if (customerId != null) {
            q.eq(Order::getCustomerId, customerId);
        }
        if (businessType != null && !businessType.isBlank()) {
            q.eq(Order::getBusinessType, businessType);
        }
        if (orderNo != null && !orderNo.isBlank()) {
            q.like(Order::getOrderNo, orderNo);
        }

        Page<Order> result = orderMapper.selectPage(new Page<>(pageNum, effectiveSize), q);
        List<OrderView> views = result.getRecords().stream().map(this::toView).toList();
        return PageResult.of(views, result.getTotal(), pageNum, effectiveSize);
    }

    @Override
    public OrderDetailView get(Long id) {
        Order order = requireOrder(id);
        return toDetail(order);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ 门户端

    @Override
    public PageResult<OrderView> pageByCustomer(Long customerId, long current, long size, String status) {
        return page(current, size, status, customerId, null, null);
    }

    @Override
    public OrderDetailView getOwned(Long id, Long customerId) {
        Order order = orderMapper.selectById(id);
        // 归属不符一律按「不存在」处理 —— 不能泄露「存在但不属于你」
        if (order == null || !customerId.equals(order.getCustomerId())) {
            throw new OrderException(OrderErrorCode.ORDER_NOT_FOUND);
        }
        return toDetail(order);
    }

    /**
     * 客户自助建单。
     *
     * <p><b>customerId 一律以本方法的入参为准</b> —— 门户端的 DTO 里根本没有 customerId 字段,
     * 客户端无法指定归属; 这里再强制覆盖一次, 保证「客户只能给自己建单」。
     */
    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderView createForCustomer(Long customerId, CreateOrderRequest request) {
        if (customerId == null) {
            throw new OrderException(OrderErrorCode.CUSTOMER_NOT_FOUND, "缺少客户身份");
        }
        return createInternal(request, customerId, OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT);
    }

    // ------------------------------------------------------------ 内部

    /**
     * @param customerIdOverride 非空时**覆盖** {@code request.customerId()} (门户端路径);
     *                           为空时用 {@code request.customerId()} (管理端路径)。
     */
    private OrderView createInternal(CreateOrderRequest request, Long customerIdOverride, String status) {
        List<CargoRequest> cargo = requireCargo(request);
        List<ServiceItemRequest> items = requireServiceItems(request);

        Order order = new Order();
        applyHeader(order, request, customerIdOverride);
        order.setOrderNo(numberGenerator.generate(OrderConstants.NUMBER_RULE_CODE));
        order.setOrderStatus(status);
        order.setSettlementCurrency(
                request.settlementCurrency() == null || request.settlementCurrency().isBlank()
                        ? "CNY" : request.settlementCurrency());
        order.setIsOversized(Boolean.TRUE.equals(request.isOversized()) ? 1 : 0);
        orderMapper.insert(order);

        replaceChildren(order.getId(), cargo, items);
        return toView(order);
    }

    private List<CargoRequest> requireCargo(CreateOrderRequest request) {
        if (request.cargo() == null || request.cargo().isEmpty()) {
            throw new OrderException(OrderErrorCode.CARGO_EMPTY);
        }
        return request.cargo();
    }

    private List<ServiceItemRequest> requireServiceItems(CreateOrderRequest request) {
        if (request.serviceItems() == null || request.serviceItems().isEmpty()) {
            throw new OrderException(OrderErrorCode.SERVICE_ITEM_EMPTY);
        }
        return request.serviceItems();
    }

    private void applyHeader(Order order, CreateOrderRequest request, Long customerIdOverride) {
        order.setCustomerId(customerIdOverride != null ? customerIdOverride : request.customerId());
        order.setBusinessType(request.businessType());
        order.setTradeDirection(request.tradeDirection());
        order.setIncoterms(request.incoterms());
        order.setServiceType(request.serviceType());
        order.setOriginAddress(request.originAddress());
        order.setDestAddress(request.destAddress());
        order.setCargoReadyDate(request.cargoReadyDate());
        order.setEtd(request.etd());
        order.setEta(request.eta());
        order.setContainerQty(request.containerQty());
        order.setContainerTypeCode(request.containerTypeCode());
        order.setMarksAndNos(request.marksAndNos());
        order.setCargoDescription(request.cargoDescription());
        order.setTemperatureRange(request.temperatureRange());
        order.setRemark(request.remark());
    }

    /**
     * 用明细整体替换子表, 然后把聚合值回写主表。
     *
     * <p>先软删旧的再插新的 —— 简单且不会留下孤儿行; 委托单明细量小 (几十行),
     * 整表替换的写放大可以接受。
     */
    private void replaceChildren(Long orderId, List<CargoRequest> cargo, List<ServiceItemRequest> items) {
        cargoMapper.delete(new LambdaQueryWrapper<OrderCargo>().eq(OrderCargo::getOrderId, orderId));
        serviceItemMapper.delete(
                new LambdaQueryWrapper<OrderServiceItem>().eq(OrderServiceItem::getOrderId, orderId));

        BigDecimal totalQty = BigDecimal.ZERO;
        BigDecimal totalWeight = BigDecimal.ZERO;
        BigDecimal totalVolume = BigDecimal.ZERO;
        boolean anyDangerous = false;

        for (CargoRequest r : cargo) {
            OrderCargo row = new OrderCargo();
            row.setOrderId(orderId);
            row.setCargoNameCn(r.cargoNameCn());
            row.setCargoNameEn(r.cargoNameEn());
            row.setHsCode(r.hsCode());
            row.setPkgType(r.pkgType());
            row.setPkgQty(nz(r.pkgQty()));
            row.setUnit(r.unit());
            row.setWeightKg(nz(r.weightKg()));
            row.setNetWeightKg(r.netWeightKg());
            row.setVolumeCbm(nz(r.volumeCbm()));
            row.setLengthCm(r.lengthCm());
            row.setWidthCm(r.widthCm());
            row.setHeightCm(r.heightCm());
            row.setIsStackable(r.isStackable() == null ? 1 : (r.isStackable() ? 1 : 0));
            row.setCargoValue(r.cargoValue());
            row.setCurrencyCode(r.currencyCode());
            row.setBrandName(r.brandName());
            row.setModel(r.model());
            row.setIsDangerous(Boolean.TRUE.equals(r.isDangerous()) ? 1 : 0);
            row.setUnNo(r.unNo());
            row.setDangerClass(r.dangerClass());
            row.setRemark(r.remark());
            cargoMapper.insert(row);

            totalQty = totalQty.add(nz(r.pkgQty()));
            totalWeight = totalWeight.add(nz(r.weightKg()));
            totalVolume = totalVolume.add(nz(r.volumeCbm()));
            anyDangerous = anyDangerous || Boolean.TRUE.equals(r.isDangerous());
        }

        for (ServiceItemRequest r : items) {
            OrderServiceItem row = new OrderServiceItem();
            row.setOrderId(orderId);
            row.setFeeType(r.feeType());
            row.setFeeName(r.feeName());
            row.setFeeCategory(r.feeCategory());
            row.setCalcBasis(r.calcBasis());
            row.setQty(nz(r.qty()));
            row.setUnitPrice(nz(r.unitPrice()));
            row.setAmount(nz(r.amount()));
            row.setCurrencyCode(r.currencyCode() == null || r.currencyCode().isBlank()
                    ? "CNY" : r.currencyCode());
            row.setSupplierId(r.supplierId());
            row.setCostAmount(r.costAmount());
            row.setIsMandatory(Boolean.TRUE.equals(r.isMandatory()) ? 1 : 0);
            row.setSource(OrderConstants.SRC_MANUAL);
            row.setRemark(r.remark());
            serviceItemMapper.insert(row);
        }

        Order agg = new Order();
        agg.setId(orderId);
        agg.setTotalQty(totalQty);
        agg.setTotalWeightKg(totalWeight);
        agg.setTotalVolumeCbm(totalVolume);
        agg.setIsDangerous(anyDangerous ? 1 : 0);
        orderMapper.updateById(agg);
    }

    private Order requireOrder(Long id) {
        Order order = orderMapper.selectById(id);
        if (order == null) {
            throw new OrderException(OrderErrorCode.ORDER_NOT_FOUND);
        }
        return order;
    }

    private OrderView toView(Order o) {
        return new OrderView(
                o.getId(), o.getOrderNo(), o.getCustomerId(), o.getBusinessType(),
                o.getTradeDirection(), o.getIncoterms(), o.getServiceType(),
                o.getOriginAddress(), o.getDestAddress(),
                o.getTotalQty(), o.getTotalWeightKg(), o.getTotalVolumeCbm(),
                o.getOrderStatus(), o.getSettlementCurrency(),
                o.getEtd(), o.getEta(), o.getCreatedAt());
    }

    private OrderDetailView toDetail(Order o) {
        List<OrderCargo> cargoRows = cargoMapper.selectList(
                new LambdaQueryWrapper<OrderCargo>().eq(OrderCargo::getOrderId, o.getId()));
        List<OrderServiceItem> itemRows = serviceItemMapper.selectList(
                new LambdaQueryWrapper<OrderServiceItem>().eq(OrderServiceItem::getOrderId, o.getId()));

        List<CargoView> cargoViews = new ArrayList<>();
        for (OrderCargo c : cargoRows) {
            cargoViews.add(new CargoView(c.getId(), c.getCargoNameCn(), c.getCargoNameEn(),
                    c.getHsCode(), c.getPkgType(), c.getPkgQty(), c.getUnit(), c.getWeightKg(),
                    c.getNetWeightKg(), c.getVolumeCbm(), c.getLengthCm(), c.getWidthCm(),
                    c.getHeightCm(), c.getIsStackable(), c.getCargoValue(), c.getCurrencyCode(),
                    c.getBrandName(), c.getModel(), c.getIsDangerous(), c.getUnNo(),
                    c.getDangerClass(), c.getRemark()));
        }
        List<ServiceItemView> itemViews = new ArrayList<>();
        for (OrderServiceItem s : itemRows) {
            itemViews.add(new ServiceItemView(s.getId(), s.getFeeType(), s.getFeeName(),
                    s.getFeeCategory(), s.getCalcBasis(), s.getQty(), s.getUnitPrice(),
                    s.getAmount(), s.getCurrencyCode(), s.getSupplierId(), s.getCostAmount(),
                    s.getIsMandatory(), s.getSource(), s.getRemark()));
        }

        return new OrderDetailView(
                o.getId(), o.getOrderNo(), o.getCustomerId(), o.getBusinessType(),
                o.getTradeDirection(), o.getIncoterms(), o.getServiceType(),
                o.getOriginAddress(), o.getDestAddress(),
                o.getTotalQty(), o.getTotalWeightKg(), o.getTotalVolumeCbm(),
                o.getOrderStatus(), o.getSettlementCurrency(),
                o.getEtd(), o.getEta(), o.getCreatedAt(),
                o.getCargoReadyDate(), o.getContainerQty(), o.getContainerTypeCode(),
                o.getMarksAndNos(), o.getCargoDescription(), o.getIsDangerous(),
                o.getIsOversized(), o.getTemperatureRange(), o.getRemark(),
                o.getCancelReason(), o.getCloseReason(), o.getVoidReason(),
                o.getSubmittedAt(), o.getConfirmedAt(), o.getDeliveredAt(),
                o.getSettledAt(), o.getClosedAt(), o.getCreatedBy(), o.getUpdatedBy(),
                cargoViews, itemViews, List.of());
    }

    private static BigDecimal nz(Double v) {
        return v == null ? BigDecimal.ZERO : BigDecimal.valueOf(v);
    }
}
```

> **`createdBy` / `updatedBy` 本轮留空**（列可空）。
> 本仓 `TenantContext` 只有一个 `ThreadLocal<Long>`, 值是 **tenantId** 而不是 userId ——
> 拿它填「谁建的」是**错的**。等真要记操作人时, 再从
> `SecurityContextHolder` 里取 `RbacUserDetails.getUserId()`。所以上面代码里**没有** `currentUserId()` 方法,
> 也没有任何 `setCreatedBy` / `setUpdatedBy` 调用 —— 实现时不要"顺手补上"。

> **注意**：事件发布走的是 **`EventBus`**（`com.lumen.extension.outbox.EventBus`），
> **不是** `ApplicationEventPublisher`。`DefaultEventBus.publish` 会在同一事务里写
> `t_event_outbox` 行 + 再转发给 `ApplicationEventPublisher` —— 直接注入
> `ApplicationEventPublisher` 会**绕过 outbox**，事件不会落库，finance 侧也收不到。
> 单元测试 mock 的必须是 `EventBus`。


在文件末尾追加用例：

```java
    private Order existingOrder(String status) {
        Order o = new Order();
        o.setId(100L);
        o.setOrderNo("ORD-20261110-000001");
        o.setOrderStatus(status);
        o.setCustomerId(42L);
        o.setSettlementCurrency("CNY");
        o.setTotalQty(BigDecimal.TEN);
        o.setTotalWeightKg(BigDecimal.valueOf(1000));
        o.setTotalVolumeCbm(BigDecimal.valueOf(12.5));
        return o;
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("submit: DRAFT → PENDING_CONFIRM, 发一条 OrderStateChangedEvent")
    void submit_draftToPendingConfirm_publishesEvent() {
        Order o = existingOrder(OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT);
        when(orderMapper.selectById(100L)).thenReturn(o);
        lenient().when(stateMachineEngine.allowedNext(OrderConstants.STATE_MACHINE_CODE,
                OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT)).thenReturn(List.of(OrderConstants.STATUS_PENDING_CONFIRM));

        service.submit(100L);

        ArgumentCaptor<OrderStateChangedEvent> captor =
                ArgumentCaptor.forClass(OrderStateChangedEvent.class);
        verify(eventBus).publish(captor.capture());
        OrderStateChangedEvent ev = captor.getValue();
        assertThat(ev.getFromState()).isEqualTo(OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT);
        assertThat(ev.getToState()).isEqualTo(OrderConstants.STATUS_PENDING_CONFIRM);
        assertThat(ev.getAction()).isEqualTo("submit");
        assertThat(ev.getOrderId()).isEqualTo(100L);
        assertThat(ev.getCustomerId()).isEqualTo(42L);
        assertThat(ev.eventType()).isEqualTo("order.state.changed");
        assertThat(ev.aggregateType()).isEqualTo("ORDER");
        assertThat(ev.aggregateId()).isEqualTo("100");
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("submit: 非法转换 → OrderException(STATE_TRANSITION_INVALID) 409, 不是 StateMachineException")
    void submit_illegalTransition_throws409() {
        Order o = existingOrder(OrderConstants.STATUS_SETTLED);
        when(orderMapper.selectById(100L)).thenReturn(o);
        lenient().when(stateMachineEngine.allowedNext(OrderConstants.STATE_MACHINE_CODE,
                OrderConstants.STATUS_SETTLED)).thenReturn(List.of(OrderConstants.STATUS_CLOSED));

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> service.submit(100L))
                .isInstanceOf(OrderException.class)
                .extracting(e -> ((OrderException) e).getErrorCode())
                .isEqualTo(OrderErrorCode.STATE_TRANSITION_INVALID);

        // 关键: 不能让 StateMachineException (14001/400) 逃出去
        assertThatThrownBy(() -> service.submit(100L))
                .isNotInstanceOf(com.lumen.extension.state.StateMachineException.class);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("cancel: 原因空白 → OrderException(REASON_REQUIRED)")
    void cancel_blankReason_throws() {
        Order o = existingOrder(OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT);
        when(orderMapper.selectById(100L)).thenReturn(o);

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> service.cancel(100L, "   "))
                .isInstanceOf(OrderException.class)
                .extracting(e -> ((OrderException) e).getErrorCode())
                .isEqualTo(OrderErrorCode.REASON_REQUIRED);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("confirm: 服务项金额合计为 0 → OrderException(AMOUNT_INVALID)")
    void confirm_zeroAmount_throws() {
        Order o = existingOrder(OrderConstants.STATUS_PENDING_CONFIRM);
        when(orderMapper.selectById(100L)).thenReturn(o);
        OrderServiceItem s = new OrderServiceItem();
        s.setId(2L); s.setOrderId(100L); s.setAmount(BigDecimal.ZERO);
        when(serviceItemMapper.selectList(any(LambdaQueryWrapper.class))).thenReturn(List.of(s));
        lenient().when(stateMachineEngine.allowedNext(OrderConstants.STATE_MACHINE_CODE,
                OrderConstants.STATUS_PENDING_CONFIRM)).thenReturn(List.of(OrderConstants.STATUS_CONFIRMED));

        assertThatThrownBy(() -> service.confirm(100L))
                .isInstanceOf(OrderException.class)
                .extracting(e -> ((OrderException) e).getErrorCode())
                .isEqualTo(OrderErrorCode.AMOUNT_INVALID);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("get: allowedNextStates 来自 StateMachineEngine.allowedNext")
    void get_allowedNextStatesFromEngine() {
        Order o = existingOrder(OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT);
        when(orderMapper.selectById(100L)).thenReturn(o);
        lenient().when(cargoMapper.selectList(any(LambdaQueryWrapper.class))).thenReturn(List.of());
        lenient().when(serviceItemMapper.selectList(any(LambdaQueryWrapper.class))).thenReturn(List.of());
        lenient().when(stateMachineEngine.allowedNext(OrderConstants.STATE_MACHINE_CODE,
                OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT)).thenReturn(
                List.of(OrderConstants.STATUS_PENDING_CONFIRM, OrderConstants.STATUS_CANCELLED,
                        OrderConstants.STATUS_VOID));

        OrderDetailView view = service.get(100L);
        assertThat(view.allowedNextStates()).containsExactlyInAnyOrder(
                OrderConstants.STATUS_PENDING_CONFIRM, OrderConstants.STATUS_CANCELLED,
                OrderConstants.STATUS_VOID);
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 建 `OrderStateChangedEvent`**

Create `lumen-parent/lumen-order/src/main/java/com/lumen/order/event/OrderStateChangedEvent.java`：

```java
package com.lumen.order.event;

import com.lumen.extension.outbox.DomainEvent;
import lombok.Getter;

import java.math.BigDecimal;
import java.util.Objects;

/**
 * 委托单状态变更事件 (Iteration 4.6).
 *
 * <p><b>eventType 单一 + action 字段区分语义</b> —— 设计文档 (05v4) 用的是
 * {@code order.submitted} / {@code order.confirmed} 等多个 eventType, 但
 * {@code DomainEvent.eventType()} 是单值契约, 做多个子类会显著放大代码面。
 * 本类用一个 {@code eventType = "order.state.changed"} + {@code action} 承载全部 9 个动作
 * (submit / confirm / return / cancel / void / start_operation / deliver / settle / close)。
 *
 * <p><b>被 {@code lumen-finance} 反射消费</b> —— finance <b>不依赖</b> lumen-order,
 * 它按 {@code event.getClass().getSimpleName()} 判定类型、用反射取 getter
 * (照 {@code BusinessEventListener.mapDeliveryOrderEvent} 的既有套路)。
 * 所以<b>字段名不要随便改</b> —— finance 侧按 {@code getToState} / {@code getOrderId} /
 * {@code getCustomerId} / {@code getTotalWeightKg} / {@code getTotalVolumeCbm} /
 * {@code getTotalQty} / {@code getEstimatedAmount} 取值, 改了名字 finance 侧会静默拿到 null。
 *
 * <p><b>getter 命名必须是 {@code getXxx}</b> —— finance 侧反射是
 * {@code cls.getMethod("getToState")}, {@code getMethod} 只认 {@code get} 前缀的公有方法。
 * 照 {@code DeliveryOrderStateChangedEvent} 的写法: 类上打 {@code @Getter},
 * 字段即可自动生成 {@code getXxx()}; 基类 {@code DomainEvent} 自己的
 * {@code eventType()} / {@code aggregateType()} / {@code aggregateId()} / {@code tenantId()}
 * 是<b>无 get 前缀</b> 的抽象方法, 由子类覆写。
 */
@Getter
public class OrderStateChangedEvent extends DomainEvent {

    private final Long tenantId;
    private final Long orderId;
    private final String orderNo;
    private final String fromState;
    private final String toState;
    private final String action;
    private final Long customerId;
    private final BigDecimal totalWeightKg;
    private final BigDecimal totalVolumeCbm;
    private final BigDecimal totalQty;
    private final BigDecimal estimatedAmount;
    private final String currencyCode;
    private final String actor;

    public OrderStateChangedEvent(Long tenantId, Long orderId, String orderNo,
                                   String fromState, String toState, String action,
                                   Long customerId, BigDecimal totalWeightKg,
                                   BigDecimal totalVolumeCbm, BigDecimal totalQty,
                                   BigDecimal estimatedAmount, String currencyCode, String actor) {
        Objects.requireNonNull(tenantId, "tenantId");
        Objects.requireNonNull(orderId, "orderId");
        Objects.requireNonNull(toState, "toState");
        this.tenantId = tenantId;
        this.orderId = orderId;
        this.orderNo = orderNo;
        this.fromState = fromState;
        this.toState = toState;
        this.action = action;
        this.customerId = customerId;
        this.totalWeightKg = totalWeightKg;
        this.totalVolumeCbm = totalVolumeCbm;
        this.totalQty = totalQty;
        this.estimatedAmount = estimatedAmount;
        this.currencyCode = currencyCode;
        this.actor = actor;
    }

    @Override
    public String eventType() {
        return "order.state.changed";
    }

    @Override
    public String aggregateType() {
        return "ORDER";
    }

    /** {@code DomainEvent.aggregateId()} 的签名是 {@code String} —— 必须转字符串。 */
    @Override
    public String aggregateId() {
        return String.valueOf(orderId);
    }

    @Override
    public Long tenantId() {
        return tenantId;
    }
}
```

> **已核实（不要照着旧写法写）**：
> - `DomainEvent` 基类**没有** `super(tenantId)` 构造器 —— 它只有隐式无参构造，
>   `tenantId` 必须由子类**自己存字段**并覆写 `tenantId()` 返回。
> - `DomainEvent.aggregateId()` 的返回类型是 **`String`**，不是 `Long`。
> - 基类的 `eventType()` / `aggregateType()` / `aggregateId()` / `tenantId()` 是
>   **无 get 前缀**的抽象方法；业务字段才用 `@Getter` 生成 `getXxx()`。
>
> 上面的类**必须**提供 `getToState()` / `getOrderId()` / `getCustomerId()` /
> `getTotalWeightKg()` / `getTotalVolumeCbm()` / `getTotalQty()` / `getEstimatedAmount()`
> 这 7 个 `get` 前缀的 getter（finance 侧按这些名字反射）—— `@Getter` 已经全部生成。

- [ ] **Step 4: 在 `OrderServiceImpl` 里实现状态转换**

在 `OrderServiceImpl` 的字段区补两个依赖：

```java
    private final StateMachineEngine stateMachineEngine;
    private final EventBus eventBus;
```

并在 import 区补：

```java
import com.lumen.extension.outbox.EventBus;
import com.lumen.extension.state.StateMachineEngine;
```

> **事件发布用 `EventBus`（`com.lumen.extension.outbox.EventBus`）不是 `ApplicationEventPublisher`** ——
> `DefaultEventBus.publish` 会在**同一事务内**写 `t_event_outbox` 行 + `ApplicationEventPublisher.publishEvent`，
> 这正是「同事务写 outbox」的既有机制。`EventBus.publish` 是 `Propagation.MANDATORY`，
> 所以调用方（转换方法）**必须是 `@Transactional`**。

在 `OrderServiceImpl` 里加转换实现（每个都走同一条 `transition` 收口）：

```java
    // ------------------------------------------------------------ 状态转换

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderDetailView submit(Long id) {
        Order o = requireOrder(id);
        requireTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_PENDING_CONFIRM, "submit");
        requireBasicsComplete(o);
        o.setSubmittedAt(LocalDateTime.now());
        return applyTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_PENDING_CONFIRM, "submit", null);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderDetailView confirm(Long id) {
        Order o = requireOrder(id);
        requireTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_CONFIRMED, "confirm");
        requirePositiveAmount(o);
        o.setConfirmedAt(LocalDateTime.now());
        return applyTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_CONFIRMED, "confirm", null);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderDetailView returnToDraft(Long id, String reason) {
        Order o = requireOrder(id);
        requireReason(reason);
        requireTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT, "return");
        return applyTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT, "return", reason);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderDetailView cancel(Long id, String reason) {
        Order o = requireOrder(id);
        requireReason(reason);
        requireTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_CANCELLED, "cancel");
        o.setCancelReason(reason);
        return applyTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_CANCELLED, "cancel", reason);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderDetailView voidOrder(Long id, String reason) {
        Order o = requireOrder(id);
        requireReason(reason);
        requireTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_VOID, "void");
        o.setVoidReason(reason);
        return applyTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_VOID, "void", reason);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderDetailView startOperation(Long id) {
        Order o = requireOrder(id);
        requireTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_IN_OPERATION, "start_operation");
        return applyTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_IN_OPERATION, "start_operation", null);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderDetailView deliver(Long id) {
        Order o = requireOrder(id);
        requireTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_DELIVERED, "deliver");
        o.setDeliveredAt(LocalDateTime.now());
        return applyTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_DELIVERED, "deliver", null);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderDetailView settle(Long id) {
        Order o = requireOrder(id);
        requireTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_SETTLED, "settle");
        o.setSettledAt(LocalDateTime.now());
        return applyTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_SETTLED, "settle", null);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderDetailView close(Long id, String reason) {
        Order o = requireOrder(id);
        requireTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_CLOSED, "close");
        o.setCloseReason(reason);
        o.setClosedAt(LocalDateTime.now());
        return applyTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_CLOSED, "close", reason);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderDetailView confirmOwned(Long id, Long customerId) {
        Order o = requireOwned(id, customerId);
        requireTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_CONFIRMED, "confirm");
        requirePositiveAmount(o);
        o.setConfirmedAt(LocalDateTime.now());
        return applyTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_CONFIRMED, "confirm", null);
    }

    @Override
    @Transactional
    public OrderDetailView cancelOwned(Long id, Long customerId, String reason) {
        Order o = requireOwned(id, customerId);
        requireReason(reason);
        requireTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_CANCELLED, "cancel");
        o.setCancelReason(reason);
        return applyTransition(o, OrderConstants.STATUS_CANCELLED, "cancel", reason);
    }
```

再加收口与守卫方法：

```java
    /**
     * 状态合法性判定 —— **必须走 {@code StateMachineEngine}**, 不看 Java 常量表。
     *
     * <p>用 {@code allowedNext} 先判 (而不是直接调 {@code assertTransition}), 因为
     * {@code assertTransition} 抛的是 {@code StateMachineException} —— code 14001 / HTTP **400**,
     * 与本轮约定的 409 / 409_101 不符。收口在这里统一转换, 客户端拿到的就是 409。
     */
    private void requireTransition(Order order, String toState, String action) {
        List<String> allowed = stateMachineEngine.allowedNext(
                OrderConstants.STATE_MACHINE_CODE, order.getOrderStatus());
        if (!allowed.contains(toState)) {
            throw new OrderException(OrderErrorCode.STATE_TRANSITION_INVALID,
                    "当前状态 " + order.getOrderStatus() + " 不允许转换到 " + toState);
        }
    }

    /** 落状态 + 发事件 + 返回详情。所有转换的唯一出口。 */
    private OrderDetailView applyTransition(Order order, String toState, String action, String reason) {
        String from = order.getOrderStatus();
        order.setOrderStatus(toState);
        orderMapper.updateById(order);

        BigDecimal estimated = serviceItemMapper.selectList(
                        new LambdaQueryWrapper<OrderServiceItem>()
                                .eq(OrderServiceItem::getOrderId, order.getId()))
                .stream()
                .map(OrderServiceItem::getAmount)
                .filter(a -> a != null)
                .reduce(BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal::add);

        eventBus.publish(new OrderStateChangedEvent(
                TenantContext.require(), order.getId(), order.getOrderNo(),
                from, toState, action, order.getCustomerId(),
                order.getTotalWeightKg(), order.getTotalVolumeCbm(), order.getTotalQty(),
                estimated, order.getSettlementCurrency(), null));

        return toDetail(order);
    }

    private void requireReason(String reason) {
        if (reason == null || reason.isBlank() || reason.trim().length() < OrderConstants.REASON_MIN_LENGTH) {
            throw new OrderException(OrderErrorCode.REASON_REQUIRED);
        }
    }

    /** submit 守卫: 必填项齐 + 至少 1 条明细 + 至少 1 条服务项。 */
    private void requireBasicsComplete(Order o) {
        if (o.getCustomerId() == null || o.getBusinessType() == null || o.getTradeDirection() == null
                || o.getIncoterms() == null || o.getServiceType() == null) {
            throw new OrderException(OrderErrorCode.CARGO_EMPTY,
                    "必填项缺失, 不能提交");
        }
        long cargoCount = cargoMapper.selectCount(
                new LambdaQueryWrapper<OrderCargo>().eq(OrderCargo::getOrderId, o.getId()));
        if (cargoCount == 0) {
            throw new OrderException(OrderErrorCode.CARGO_EMPTY);
        }
        long itemCount = serviceItemMapper.selectCount(
                new LambdaQueryWrapper<OrderServiceItem>().eq(OrderServiceItem::getOrderId, o.getId()));
        if (itemCount == 0) {
            throw new OrderException(OrderErrorCode.SERVICE_ITEM_EMPTY);
        }
    }

    /** confirm 守卫: 服务项金额合计 > 0。 */
    private void requirePositiveAmount(Order o) {
        BigDecimal total = serviceItemMapper.selectList(
                        new LambdaQueryWrapper<OrderServiceItem>()
                                .eq(OrderServiceItem::getOrderId, o.getId()))
                .stream()
                .map(OrderServiceItem::getAmount)
                .filter(a -> a != null)
                .reduce(BigDecimal.ZERO, BigDecimal::add);
        if (total.compareTo(BigDecimal.ZERO) <= 0) {
            throw new OrderException(OrderErrorCode.AMOUNT_INVALID);
        }
    }

    private Order requireOwned(Long id, Long customerId) {
        Order order = orderMapper.selectById(id);
        if (order == null || !customerId.equals(order.getCustomerId())) {
            throw new OrderException(OrderErrorCode.ORDER_NOT_FOUND);
        }
        return order;
    }
```

并把 `toDetail` 里的 `List.of()` 改成：

```java

                OrderConstants.STATE_MACHINE_CODE, o.getOrderStatus());
        ...
                cargoViews, itemViews, allowedNext);
```

```java
package com.lumen.order.controller;

import com.lumen.common.api.PageResult;
import com.lumen.order.OrderConstants;
import com.lumen.order.dto.OrderDetailView;
import com.lumen.order.dto.OrderView;
import com.lumen.order.service.OrderService;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.extension.ExtendWith;
import org.mockito.InjectMocks;
import org.mockito.Mock;
import org.mockito.junit.jupiter.MockitoExtension;

import java.util.List;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.any;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.anyLong;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.eq;
import static org.mockito.ArgumentMatchers.isNull;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.verify;
import static org.mockito.Mockito.when;

/**
 * OrderController wire contract 单测 (Iteration 4.6).
 *
 * <p>照 {@code CsExceptionControllerTest} 的做法: 直接 new controller + 调方法,
 * 不用 MockMvc —— 锁的是「参数名 / 返回信封 / 分页口径」这些前端真正依赖的形状。
 */
@ExtendWith(MockitoExtension.class)
@DisplayName("OrderController 单元测试 (wire contract)")
class OrderControllerTest {

    @Mock private OrderService service;

    @InjectMocks
    private OrderController controller;

    @Test
    @DisplayName("page: 分页参数名 current/size, 返回 R<PageResult<OrderView>>")
    void page_usesCurrentAndSize() {
        OrderView v = new OrderView(1L, "ORD-20261110-000001", 42L, "OCEAN_FCL", "EXPORT",
                "FOB", "D2D", "上海", "洛杉矶", null, null, null,
                OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT, "CNY", null, null, null);
        when(service.page(anyLong(), anyLong(), isNull(), isNull(), isNull(), isNull()))
                .thenReturn(PageResult.of(List.of(v), 1, 1, 20));

        var res = controller.page(1L, 20L, null, null, null, null);

        assertThat(res.getCode()).isZero();
        assertThat(res.getData().getRecords()).hasSize(1);
        assertThat(res.getData().getPageNum()).isEqualTo(1);
        verify(service).page(eq(1L), eq(20L), isNull(), isNull(), isNull(), isNull());
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("get: 返回 R<OrderDetailView>")
    void get_returnsDetail() {
        OrderDetailView d = new OrderDetailView(1L, "ORD-20261110-000001", 42L, "OCEAN_FCL",
                "EXPORT", "FOB", "D2D", null, null, null, null, null,
                OrderConstants.STATUS_DRAFT, "CNY", null, null, null,
                null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null, null,
                null, null, null, null, null, null, null,
                List.of(), List.of(), List.of(OrderConstants.STATUS_PENDING_CONFIRM));
        when(service.get(1L)).thenReturn(d);

        var res = controller.get(1L);

        assertThat(res.getCode()).isZero();
        assertThat(res.getData().orderNo()).isEqualTo("ORD-20261110-000001");
        assertThat(res.getData().allowedNextStates())
                .containsExactly(OrderConstants.STATUS_PENDING_CONFIRM);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 建 `OrderController`**

Create `lumen-parent/lumen-order/src/main/java/com/lumen/order/controller/OrderController.java`：

```java
package com.lumen.order.controller;

import com.lumen.common.api.PageResult;
import com.lumen.common.api.R;
import com.lumen.order.dto.CreateOrderRequest;
import com.lumen.order.dto.OrderDetailView;
import com.lumen.order.dto.OrderView;
import com.lumen.order.service.OrderService;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import org.springframework.security.access.prepost.PreAuthorize;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PutMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestParam;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;

import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 管理端货运委托单控制器 (Iteration 4.6).
 *
 * <p>路径前缀 {@code /api/v1} —— 与既有管理端控制器一致
 * ({@code /api/v1/finance/accruals} / {@code /api/v1/dispatch/orders} / {@code /api/v1/wms/customers})。
 *
 * <p>分页参数名用 {@code current} / {@code size}（不是 pageNum / pageSize）——
 * 与 {@code CsExceptionController} / {@code SysAuditLogController} 一致。
 *
 * <p>异常统一由 {@code GlobalExceptionHandler.handleBiz} 处理
 * （{@code OrderException extends BizException}），本类不写 try/catch。
 */
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/v1/orders")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class OrderController {

    private final OrderService service;

    @GetMapping
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('order:view')")
    public R<PageResult<OrderView>> page(
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "1") long current,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "20") long size,
            @RequestParam(required = false) String status,
            @RequestParam(required = false) Long customerId,
            @RequestParam(required = false) String orderNo,
            @RequestParam(required = false) String businessType) {
        return R.ok(service.page(current, size, status, customerId, orderNo, businessType));
    }

    @GetMapping("/{id}")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('order:view')")
    public R<OrderDetailView> get(@PathVariable Long id) {
        return R.ok(service.get(id));
    }

    @PostMapping
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('order:create')")
    public R<OrderView> create(@Valid @RequestBody CreateOrderRequest request) {
        return R.ok(service.create(request));
    }

    @PutMapping("/{id}")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('order:edit')")
    public R<OrderDetailView> update(@PathVariable Long id,
                                      @Valid @RequestBody CreateOrderRequest request) {
        return R.ok(service.update(id, request));
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/submit")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('order:submit')")
    public R<OrderDetailView> submit(@PathVariable Long id) {
        return R.ok(service.submit(id));
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/confirm")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('order:confirm')")
    public R<OrderDetailView> confirm(@PathVariable Long id) {
        return R.ok(service.confirm(id));
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/return")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('order:submit')")
    public R<OrderDetailView> returnToDraft(@PathVariable Long id,
                                             @RequestBody Map<String, String> body) {
        return R.ok(service.returnToDraft(id, body == null ? null : body.get("reason")));
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/cancel")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('order:cancel')")
    public R<OrderDetailView> cancel(@PathVariable Long id,
                                     @RequestBody Map<String, String> body) {
        return R.ok(service.cancel(id, body == null ? null : body.get("reason")));
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/void")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('order:void')")
    public R<OrderDetailView> voidOrder(@PathVariable Long id,
                                         @RequestBody Map<String, String> body) {
        return R.ok(service.voidOrder(id, body == null ? null : body.get("reason")));
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/start-operation")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('order:operate')")
    public R<OrderDetailView> startOperation(@PathVariable Long id) {
        return R.ok(service.startOperation(id));
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/deliver")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('order:operate')")
    public R<OrderDetailView> deliver(@PathVariable Long id) {
        return R.ok(service.deliver(id));
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/settle")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('order:settle')")
    public R<OrderDetailView> settle(@PathVariable Long id) {
        return R.ok(service.settle(id));
    }

    @PostMapping("/{id}/close")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('order:close')")
    public R<OrderDetailView> close(@PathVariable Long id,
                                    @RequestBody Map<String, String> body) {
        return R.ok(service.close(id, body == null ? null : body.get("reason")));
    }
}
```

> **13 个端点与权限码的对应**（`@PreAuthorize` 的 authority 必须与 V42 seed 的 code 逐字一致）：
>
> | 端点 | authority |
> |---|---|
> | `GET /api/v1/orders`、`GET /api/v1/orders/{id}` | `order:view` |
> | `POST /api/v1/orders` | `order:create` |
> | `PUT /api/v1/orders/{id}` | `order:edit` |
> | `POST /api/v1/orders/{id}/submit`、`/return` | `order:submit` |
> | `POST /api/v1/orders/{id}/confirm` | `order:confirm` |
> | `POST /api/v1/orders/{id}/cancel` | `order:cancel` |
> | `POST /api/v1/orders/{id}/void` | `order:void` |
> | `POST /api/v1/orders/{id}/start-operation`、`/deliver` | `order:operate` |
> | `POST /api/v1/orders/{id}/settle` | `order:settle` |
> | `POST /api/v1/orders/{id}/close` | `order:close` |
>
> **「带 reason 的 4 个端点」**（return / cancel / void / close）用
> `@RequestBody Map<String, String>` 而不是专门的 DTO —— 理由：这 4 个端点的 body
> 只有 `{"reason": "..."}` 一个键，为它建 4 个 record 是纯噪音。reason 的长度校验
> （>= 5 字）在 service 层用 `OrderConstants.REASON_MIN_LENGTH` 做，返回
> `400_103 REASON_REQUIRED`。

## Task 6: finance 侧接 `OrderStateChangedEvent` → 交付即计费

**Files:**
- Modify: `lumen-parent/lumen-finance/src/main/java/com/lumen/finance/FinanceConstants.java`
- Modify: `lumen-parent/lumen-finance/src/main/java/com/lumen/finance/listener/BusinessEventListener.java`
- Test: `lumen-parent/lumen-finance/src/test/java/com/lumen/finance/listener/BusinessEventListenerTest.java`

**为什么放在 Task 6 而不是 Task 4**：Task 4 只负责「事件发出去」，Task 6 负责「finance 收到并计费」。
两者之间的契约是**反射**（finance 不依赖 lumen-order），所以必须有一个单测锁住
「finance 按 `getXxx()` 读到了正确的值」—— 这个单测就是本 Task 的核心。

- [ ] **Step 1: 写失败测试 —— finance 能读到 Order 事件并映射成计费 DTO**

在 `BusinessEventListenerTest.java` 的 import 区补：

```java
import com.lumen.finance.FinanceConstants;
```

> `FinanceConstants` 已经在 import 区了（既有测试用了 `FinanceConstants.BIZ_LAST_MILE`），
> **不要重复加**。

在文件末尾的 `test stubs` 段之前插入 2 个用例：

```java
    // ---------- 4. OrderStateChangedEvent DELIVERED → engine.accrue 被调用 ----------

    @Test
    @DisplayName("OrderStateChangedEvent DELIVERED → engine.accrue(DTO) 被调用, 字段映射正确")
    void onEvent_orderDelivered_callsAccrue() {
        OrderStateChangedEvent event = new OrderStateChangedEvent(
            1001L,                                    // tenantId
            "DELIVERED",                              // toState
            555L,                                     // orderId
            42L,                                      // customerId
            new BigDecimal("1200"),                   // totalWeightKg
            new BigDecimal("12.5"),                   // totalVolumeCbm
            new BigDecimal("88"),                     // totalQty
            new BigDecimal("8600.00")                 // estimatedAmount
        );

        listener.onEvent(event);

        ArgumentCaptor<BillingEventDto> cap = ArgumentCaptor.forClass(BillingEventDto.class);
        verify(engine).accrue(cap.capture());
        BillingEventDto dto = cap.getValue();
        assertThat(dto.tenantId()).isEqualTo(1001L);
        assertThat(dto.businessType()).isEqualTo(FinanceConstants.BIZ_ORDER);
        assertThat(dto.businessId()).isEqualTo(555L);
        assertThat(dto.direction()).isEqualTo(FinanceConstants.DIR_RECEIVABLE);
        assertThat(dto.counterpartyId()).isEqualTo(42L);
        assertThat(dto.counterpartyType()).isEqualTo(FinanceConstants.CP_CUSTOMER);
        assertThat(dto.env()).containsEntry("weight", new BigDecimal("1200"));
        assertThat(dto.env()).containsEntry("quantity", new BigDecimal("88"));
        assertThat(dto.env()).containsEntry("amount", new BigDecimal("8600.00"));
        assertThat(dto.sourceEventId()).isNotBlank();
    }

    // ---------- 5. OrderStateChangedEvent 非 DELIVERED → 静默 ----------

    @Test
    @DisplayName("OrderStateChangedEvent CONFIRMED → 静默跳过, 不调 accrue")
    void onEvent_orderNotDelivered_skipped() {
        OrderStateChangedEvent event = new OrderStateChangedEvent(
            1001L, "CONFIRMED", 555L, 42L,
            new BigDecimal("1200"), new BigDecimal("12.5"), new BigDecimal("88"),
            new BigDecimal("8600.00"));

        listener.onEvent(event);

        verify(engine, never()).accrue(any(BillingEventDto.class));
    }
```

在文件末尾的 `test stubs` 段里补一个 stub 类（**类名必须叫
`OrderStateChangedEvent`** —— listener 按 `getClass().getSimpleName()` 分派，
名字对不上就静默跳过，测试会「假绿」）：

```java
    /**
     * 委托单状态事件 stub (Iteration 4.6).
     *
     * <p>类名必须与 lumen-order 的 {@code OrderStateChangedEvent} <b>逐字相同</b> ——
     * listener 靠 {@code getClass().getSimpleName()} 分派, 名字对不上会静默跳过。
     *
     * <p>getter 全部用 {@code getXxx} 命名 —— finance 侧反射是
     * {@code cls.getMethod("getToState")}, 只认 get 前缀。
     */
    public static class OrderStateChangedEvent extends DomainEvent {
        private final Long tenantId;
        private final String toState;
        private final Long orderId;
        private final Long customerId;
        private final BigDecimal totalWeightKg;
        private final BigDecimal totalVolumeCbm;
        private final BigDecimal totalQty;
        private final BigDecimal estimatedAmount;

        public OrderStateChangedEvent(Long tenantId, String toState, Long orderId, Long customerId,
                                      BigDecimal totalWeightKg, BigDecimal totalVolumeCbm,
                                      BigDecimal totalQty, BigDecimal estimatedAmount) {
            this.tenantId = tenantId;
            this.toState = toState;
            this.orderId = orderId;
            this.customerId = customerId;
            this.totalWeightKg = totalWeightKg;
            this.totalVolumeCbm = totalVolumeCbm;
            this.totalQty = totalQty;
            this.estimatedAmount = estimatedAmount;
        }

        public Long getTenantId() { return tenantId; }
        public String getToState() { return toState; }
        public Long getOrderId() { return orderId; }
        public Long getCustomerId() { return customerId; }
        public BigDecimal getTotalWeightKg() { return totalWeightKg; }
        public BigDecimal getTotalVolumeCbm() { return totalVolumeCbm; }
        public BigDecimal getTotalQty() { return totalQty; }
        public BigDecimal getEstimatedAmount() { return estimatedAmount; }

        @Override public String eventType() { return "order.state.changed"; }
        @Override public String aggregateType() { return "ORDER"; }
        @Override public String aggregateId() { return String.valueOf(orderId); }
        @Override public Long tenantId() { return tenantId; }
    }
```

```java
    // 4.6 — 货运委托单业务类型 (lumen-order OrderConstants.ORDER 聚合的字符串)
    // 与 BIZ_LAST_MILE 同级, 独立存放: lumen-finance 不依赖 lumen-order — 仅作为比对值.
    public static final String BIZ_ORDER = "ORDER";

    // 4.6 — 委托单触发计费的终态 (lumen-order OrderConstants.STATUS_DELIVERED 字符串)
    // 交付即计费: 货已交到客户手上, 费用已确定, 此时开提单。
    public static final String FIN_TRIGGER_STATE_ORDER = "DELIVERED";
```

- [ ] **Step 4: 在 `BusinessEventListener` 里加 case + 映射方法**

在 `mapToBillingEvent` 的 switch 里加一行：

```java
        return switch (simpleName) {
            case "DeliveryOrderStateChangedEvent" -> mapDeliveryOrderEvent(event);
            case "OrderStateChangedEvent" -> mapOrderEvent(event);
            // TODO(WMS): add cases for "WmsInboundCompletedEvent" and
            //            "WmsOutboundCompletedEvent" once lumen-wms publishes them.
            default -> null;
        };
```

在 `mapDeliveryOrderEvent` 方法下面加：

```java
    /**
     * 货运委托单状态事件 → BillingEventDto (仅 DELIVERED 触发计费).
     *
     * <p>env 提取规则 (全部通过反射取 getter):
     * <ul>
     *   <li>{@code weight}   ← {@code getTotalWeightKg()} (BigDecimal)</li>
     *   <li>{@code quantity} ← {@code getTotalQty()} (件数)</li>
     *   <li>{@code amount}   ← {@code getEstimatedAmount()} (委托单服务项金额合计)</li>
     *   <li>{@code distance} ← 不提供 (委托单没有距离维度; 留空让 Aviator 走 null 分支)</li>
     * </ul>
     *
     * <p><b>env 的 key 必须落在 BillingScriptEngine 的沙箱白名单内</b>
     * (amount / weight / distance / quantity / customer_level / zone / service_type /
     * is_weekend / is_holiday) —— 超出白名单求值时抛
     * {@code FinanceException_UnknownVar}, accrual 落 OPEN_FAILED。
     *
     * <p>businessType = {@code ORDER} —— 对应 {@code t_billing_rule.business_type}。
     * 没有 ACTIVE 的 ORDER 规则时 {@code accrue} 会走 markNoRule 分支 (不写 accrual),
     * 这是预期行为, 不是 bug。
     */
    private BillingEventDto mapOrderEvent(DomainEvent event) {
        Object toState = invokeGetter(event, "getToState", "toState");
        if (!FinanceConstants.FIN_TRIGGER_STATE_ORDER.equals(toState)) {
            return null;
        }

        Map<String, Object> env = new HashMap<>();
        putIfPresent(env, "weight",   invokeGetter(event, "getTotalWeightKg", "weight"));
        putIfPresent(env, "quantity", invokeGetter(event, "getTotalQty", "quantity"));
        putIfPresent(env, "amount",   invokeGetter(event, "getEstimatedAmount", "amount"));

        Long businessId = asLong(invokeGetter(event, "getOrderId", "businessId"));
        Long counterpartyId = asLong(invokeGetter(event, "getCustomerId", "counterpartyId"));
        return new BillingEventDto(
            event.tenantId(),
            event.getEventId(),
            FinanceConstants.BIZ_ORDER,
            businessId,
            FinanceConstants.DIR_RECEIVABLE,
            counterpartyId,
            FinanceConstants.CP_CUSTOMER,
            env
        );
    }
```

- [ ] **Step 1: 先写失败测试 —— JWT 里必须带 4 个新 authority**

改 `PortalAuthServiceTest.java` 里 `login_issuedTokenCarriesMerchantBillingAuthorities`
（就是那个 `containsExactlyInAnyOrder`）—— **把断言从 11 个改成 15 个**：

```java
        assertThat(perms).containsExactlyInAnyOrder(
            "customer:track:view",
            "customer:order:create",
            "customer:order:view",
            "customer:order:cancel",
            "customer:invoice:view",

            "customer:freight_order:cancel");
```

> **为什么必须改这条测试**：`CUSTOMER_PERMS` 是**硬编码常量**，不查库。
> 只做 V42 的 `sys_permission` / `sys_role_permission` seed 而漏改这个常量，
> 门户 4 个端点会全 403 而且日志看不出原因 —— 这条断言是那道漏斗的唯一防线。
> `containsExactlyInAnyOrder` 是**精确匹配**：多一个少一个都红，别改成 `contains`。
>
> 另外：Task 1 的 V42 seed 已经把 4 个 `customer:freight_order:*` 绑给了 role 14 (customer)，
> 但门户登录**根本不走** `sys_role_permission` —— 门户 JWT 的 perms 全部来自这个常量。
> **两条通路都要有**，少任何一条都 403。

- [ ] **Step 5: 建 `PortalFreightOrderRequest`**

Create `lumen-parent/lumen-customer/src/main/java/com/lumen/customer/dto/PortalFreightOrderRequest.java`：

```java
package com.lumen.customer.dto;

import com.lumen.order.dto.CargoRequest;
import com.lumen.order.dto.ServiceItemRequest;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.NotBlank;
import jakarta.validation.constraints.Size;

import java.time.LocalDate;
import java.util.List;

/**
 * 门户端建委托单请求 (Iteration 4.6).
 *
 * <p>字段与 {@code com.lumen.order.dto.CreateOrderRequest} 完全一致,
 * <b>只是没有 {@code customerId}</b> —— 客户由 {@code PortalIdentity.requireCounterpartyId()}
 * 强制注入, body 里不该有这个字段。共用一个 DTO 会逼出「@NotNull 但门户不传」
 * 或「不标但管理端能漏传」的二选一, 两个都是坑。
 *
 * <p>{@code cargo} / {@code serviceItems} <b>不标 {@code @NotEmpty}</b> —— 空集合由
 * {@code OrderService} 抛 400_101 / 400_102, 避免同一个失败有两条码。
 */
public record PortalFreightOrderRequest(
        @NotBlank String businessType,
        @NotBlank String tradeDirection,
        @NotBlank String incoterms,
        @NotBlank String serviceType,
        @Size(max = 255) String originAddress,
        @Size(max = 255) String destAddress,
        LocalDate cargoReadyDate,
        LocalDate etd,
        LocalDate eta,
        Integer containerQty,
        @Size(max = 16) String containerTypeCode,
        @Size(max = 500) String marksAndNos,
        @Size(max = 500) String cargoDescription,
        Boolean isOversized,
        @Size(max = 32) String temperatureRange,
        String settlementCurrency,
        @Size(max = 1000) String remark,
        List<CargoRequest> cargo,
        List<ServiceItemRequest> serviceItems
) {}
```

> `CargoRequest` / `ServiceItemRequest` **直接复用** `lumen-order` 的两个 record
> （spec §六·2b 明确允许）—— `lumen-customer` 依赖 `lumen-order`，直接 import。

- [ ] **Step 6: 建 `PortalFreightOrderController`**

Create `lumen-parent/lumen-customer/src/main/java/com/lumen/customer/controller/PortalFreightOrderController.java`：

```java
package com.lumen.customer.controller;

import com.lumen.common.api.PageResult;
import com.lumen.common.api.R;
import com.lumen.customer.dto.PortalFreightOrderRequest;
import com.lumen.customer.web.PortalIdentity;
import com.lumen.order.dto.CreateOrderRequest;
import com.lumen.order.dto.OrderDetailView;
import com.lumen.order.dto.OrderView;
import com.lumen.order.dto.ServiceItemRequest;
import com.lumen.order.service.OrderService;
import jakarta.validation.Valid;
import lombok.RequiredArgsConstructor;
import lombok.extern.slf4j.Slf4j;
import org.springframework.security.access.prepost.PreAuthorize;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.GetMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PathVariable;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.PostMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestBody;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestMapping;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RequestParam;
import org.springframework.web.bind.annotation.RestController;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

/**
 * 客户门户货运委托单 controller (Iteration 4.6).
 *
 * <p>路由前缀 {@code /api/customer/freight-order} —— 与既有城配下单
 * ({@code /api/customer/order}) <b>完全分开</b>, 两者互不影响。
 * {@code CustomerContextFilter} 按 URL 前缀 {@code /api/customer/} 判定门户路由,
 * 所以这个前缀天然受门户鉴权保护。
 *
 * <p>5 个端点:
 * <ol>
 *   <li>GET  {@code /}                — {@code customer:freight_order:view}   — 我的委托单列表</li>
 *   <li>GET  {@code /{id}}            — {@code customer:freight_order:view}   — 详情 (跨客户 404)</li>
 *   <li>POST {@code /}                — {@code customer:freight_order:create} — 建单 (落 DRAFT)</li>
 *   <li>POST {@code /{id}/confirm}    — {@code customer:freight_order:confirm} — PENDING_CONFIRM → CONFIRMED</li>
 *   <li>POST {@code /{id}/cancel}     — {@code customer:freight_order:cancel}  — PENDING_CONFIRM → CANCELLED</li>
 * </ol>
 *
 * <p><b>归属边界</b>: customerId 一律来自 {@link PortalIdentity#requireCounterpartyId()},
 * <b>绝不从 body / query 读</b>。跨客户访问一律 404 (不泄露存在性)。
 *
 * <p><b>异常</b>: {@code OrderException extends BizException} → 由
 * {@code GlobalExceptionHandler.handleBiz} 翻译成 404 / 409 / 400。
 * <b>本类不新建 advice</b> (spec §六·3.1)。
 */
@Slf4j
@RestController
@RequestMapping("/api/customer/freight-order")
@RequiredArgsConstructor
public class PortalFreightOrderController {

    private final OrderService orderService;

    // ================================================================
    //  1. GET / — 我的委托单列表
    // ================================================================

    @GetMapping
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('customer:freight_order:view')")
    public R<PageResult<OrderView>> list(
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "1") long current,
            @RequestParam(defaultValue = "20") long size,
            @RequestParam(required = false) String status) {
        Long customerId = PortalIdentity.requireCounterpartyId();
        return R.ok(orderService.pageByCustomer(customerId, current, size, status));
    }

    // ================================================================
    //  2. GET /{id} — 详情
    // ================================================================

    @GetMapping("/{id}")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('customer:freight_order:view')")
    public R<OrderDetailView> detail(@PathVariable Long id) {
        Long customerId = PortalIdentity.requireCounterpartyId();
        return R.ok(orderService.getOwned(id, customerId));
    }

    // ================================================================
    //  3. POST / — 建单 (DRAFT)
    // ================================================================

    @PostMapping
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('customer:freight_order:create')")
    public R<OrderView> create(@Valid @RequestBody PortalFreightOrderRequest req) {
        Long customerId = PortalIdentity.requireCounterpartyId();
        return R.ok(orderService.createForCustomer(customerId, toCreateRequest(customerId, req)));
    }

    // ================================================================
    //  4. POST /{id}/confirm — 客户确认
    // ================================================================

    @PostMapping("/{id}/confirm")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('customer:freight_order:confirm')")
    public R<OrderDetailView> confirm(@PathVariable Long id) {
        Long customerId = PortalIdentity.requireCounterpartyId();
        return R.ok(orderService.confirmOwned(id, customerId));
    }

    // ================================================================
    //  5. POST /{id}/cancel — 客户取消
    // ================================================================

    @PostMapping("/{id}/cancel")
    @PreAuthorize("hasAuthority('customer:freight_order:cancel')")
    public R<OrderDetailView> cancel(@PathVariable Long id,
                                     @RequestBody Map<String, String> body) {
        Long customerId = PortalIdentity.requireCounterpartyId();
        return R.ok(orderService.cancelOwned(id, customerId, body == null ? null : body.get("reason")));
    }

    // ================================================================
    //  private helpers
    // ================================================================

    /**
     * 门户 DTO → 管理端 DTO。
     *
     * <p><b>customerId 用服务端取到的那个</b> —— body 里没有这个字段, 客户端也没法指定归属。
     * {@code OrderService.createForCustomer} 里还会再强制覆盖一次 (双保险)。
     *
     * <p><b>内部成本字段被抹掉</b>: {@code costAmount} (我方成本) 与 {@code supplierId}
     * (供应商) 强制置 null —— 门户端客户**不应该**能写入我方内部成本。
     */
    private CreateOrderRequest toCreateRequest(Long customerId, PortalFreightOrderRequest req) {
        List<ServiceItemRequest> items = req.serviceItems() == null
                ? List.of()
                : req.serviceItems().stream().map(this::stripInternalCost).toList();
        return new CreateOrderRequest(
                customerId,
                req.businessType(),
                req.tradeDirection(),
                req.incoterms(),
                req.serviceType(),
                req.originAddress(),
                req.destAddress(),
                req.cargoReadyDate(),
                req.etd(),
                req.eta(),
                req.containerQty(),
                req.containerTypeCode(),
                req.marksAndNos(),
                req.cargoDescription(),
                req.isOversized(),
                req.temperatureRange(),
                req.settlementCurrency(),
                req.remark(),
                req.cargo(),
                items);
    }

    /** 抹掉服务项里的内部成本字段。 */
    private ServiceItemRequest stripInternalCost(ServiceItemRequest r) {
        return new ServiceItemRequest(
                r.feeType(), r.feeName(), r.feeCategory(), r.calcBasis(),
                r.qty(), r.unitPrice(), r.amount(), r.currencyCode(),
                null, null, r.isMandatory(), r.remark());
    }
}
```

> **已知未修项（Task 11 会写进文档订正清单）**：门户**详情**端点返回的是管理端同构的
> `OrderDetailView`，其 `serviceItems: List<ServiceItemView>` **带 `costAmount` / `supplierId`**
> —— 客户能看到我方成本。spec §六·2b 明确「明细 DTO 共用」，本轮按 spec 实现，
> **只堵住了写入侧**（建单时强制置 null）。读出侧的裁剪留到下一切片
> （加门户专用详情视图 `PortalOrderDetailView`）。

> **验收命令的形式**：bootstrap 的 IT 走 **failsafe**，必须用
> `mvn -pl lumen-bootstrap verify -Dit.test='XxxIT'`。
> `mvn test -Dit.test=X` 会跑 **0 个用例** = **假绿**。
> 单条 IT 类用 `-DargLine="-Xmx3g"`；一次跑多条用 `-DargLine="-Xmx3000m"`。

```java
package com.lumen.integration;

 * {@code OrderNumberingIT} / {@code OrderBillingAccrualIT}) 共用:

 *
 * <p><b>为什么用 role 1</b>: V42 把 10 个 {@code order:*} 权限显式绑给了 role 1
 * (id 334..343)。{@code sys_role_permission} 不是「SUPER_ADMIN 自动拥有全部权限」,
 * 靠 seed 显式补行 —— 用别的角色就得多绑一批权限, 与本 IT 要验的东西无关。
 *
 * <p><b>id 段</b>: fixture 主键从 970_001 递增 (避开 PortalOrderFixtureBase 的 960_001+
 * 与 BillingPermissionIT 的 901_001+)。{@code t_order} 是 AUTO_INCREMENT,
 * 但这里仍然手工指定 id —— 便于按 id 精确清理。
 */
abstract class OrderAdminFixtureBase extends IntegrationBase {

    @BeforeEach
```
