```java
        adminUserId = nextId();
        jdbc.update(
            "INSERT INTO sys_user (id, tenant_id, username, password_hash, real_name, status, "
                + "version, deleted, created_at, updated_at) "
                + "VALUES (?, 1, ?, ?, '4.6 IT admin', 1, 0, 0, NOW(), NOW())",
            adminUserId, ADMIN_USER, passwordEncoder.encode(ADMIN_PWD));
        userIds.add(adminUserId);
        bindRole(adminUserId, SUPER_ADMIN_ROLE_ID);

        customerId = nextId();
        jdbc.update(
            "INSERT INTO wms_customer (id, tenant_id, code, name, deleted, version, created_at, updated_at) "
                + "VALUES (?, 1, ?, ?, 0, 1, NOW(), NOW())",
            customerId, CUSTOMER_CODE, CUSTOMER_NAME);
    }

    @AfterEach
    void cleanOrderFixture() {
        try {
            for (Long oid : orderIds) {
                jdbc.update("DELETE FROM t_order_cargo WHERE order_id = ?", oid);
                jdbc.update("DELETE FROM t_order_service_item WHERE order_id = ?", oid);
                jdbc.update("DELETE FROM t_event_outbox WHERE aggregate_type = 'ORDER' AND aggregate_id = ?",
                        String.valueOf(oid));
                jdbc.update("DELETE FROM t_billing_accrual WHERE business_type = 'ORDER' AND business_id = ?", oid);
                jdbc.update("DELETE FROM t_billing_event_log WHERE business_type = 'ORDER' AND business_id = ?", oid);
                jdbc.update("DELETE FROM t_order WHERE id = ?", oid);
            }
            for (Long rid : ruleIds) {
                try { jdbc.update("DELETE FROM t_billing_rule_item WHERE rule_id = ?", rid); } catch (Exception ignored) {}
                try { jdbc.update("DELETE FROM t_billing_rule WHERE id = ?", rid); } catch (Exception ignored) {}
            }
            for (Long uid : userIds) {
                try { jdbc.update("DELETE FROM sys_user_role WHERE user_id = ?", uid); } catch (Exception ignored) {}
                try { jdbc.update("DELETE FROM sys_user WHERE id = ?", uid); } catch (Exception ignored) {}
            }
            try { jdbc.update("DELETE FROM wms_customer WHERE id = ?", customerId); } catch (Exception ignored) {}
        } finally {
            orderIds.clear();
            userIds.clear();
            ruleIds.clear();
        }
    }

    // ==================================================================
    //  fixture helpers
    // ==================================================================

    protected long nextId() {
        return FIXTURE_ID.incrementAndGet();
    }

    protected void bindRole(long userId, long roleId) {
        long pk = Math.abs(System.nanoTime()) % 2_000_000_000L;
        try {
            jdbc.update(
                "INSERT INTO sys_user_role (id, user_id, role_id, tenant_id, created_at) "
                    + "VALUES (?, ?, ?, 1, NOW())", pk, userId, roleId);
        } catch (Exception dup) {
            // already bound — 幂等
        }
    }

    /** 记下一个本 IT 创建的订单 id, 供 teardown 精确清理。 */
    protected void trackOrder(long id) {
        orderIds.add(id);
    }

    /**
     * Seed 一条 ACTIVE 的 {@code t_billing_rule} (business_type='ORDER') + 单条 FORMULA item.
     *
     * <p>{@code BillingEngineServiceImpl.accrue} 只走 rule 路径, 没有 ACTIVE rule 时会
     * markNoRule 并返回 null —— 不写 accrual。priority=1 让本 IT 的 rule 在
     * {@code ORDER BY priority ASC, id ASC} 里排最前, 保证 accrue 一定能命中。
     */
    protected long seedActiveOrderRule(String expression) {
        long ruleId = nextId();
        jdbc.update(
            "INSERT INTO t_billing_rule "
                + "(id, tenant_id, business_type, direction, counterparty_type, currency, priority, "
                + " status, effective_from, effective_to, remark, created_at, updated_at, deleted, version) "
                + "VALUES (?, 1, 'ORDER', 'RECEIVABLE', 'CUSTOMER', 'CNY', 1, 'ACTIVE', "
                + " DATE_SUB(CURRENT_DATE, INTERVAL 1 DAY), NULL, '4.6 IT fixture', NOW(3), NOW(3), 0, 0)",
            ruleId);
        jdbc.update(
            "INSERT INTO t_billing_rule_item "
                + "(rule_id, sequence, item_type, expression, expression_hash, result_field, "
                + " created_at, updated_at, deleted, version) "
                + "VALUES (?, 1, 'FORMULA', ?, SHA2(?, 256), 'gross_amount', NOW(3), NOW(3), 0, 0)",
            ruleId, expression, expression);
        ruleIds.add(ruleId);
        return ruleId;
    }

    // ==================================================================
    //  HTTP helpers
    // ==================================================================

    protected String loginAdmin() {
        HttpHeaders h = new HttpHeaders();
        h.setContentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON);
        String body = "{\"tenantId\":" + TEST_TENANT
                + ",\"username\":\"" + ADMIN_USER + "\",\"password\":\"" + ADMIN_PWD + "\"}";
        ResponseEntity<R> resp = rest.exchange(
                "/api/v1/auth/login", HttpMethod.POST, new HttpEntity<>(body, h), R.class);
        assertThat(resp.getStatusCode().value()).as("admin login 应 200").isEqualTo(200);
        assertThat(resp.getBody()).isNotNull();
        assertThat(resp.getBody().getCode()).isEqualTo(0);
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
        Map<String, Object> data = (Map<String, Object>) resp.getBody().getData();
        String token = (String) data.get("accessToken");
        assertThat(token).as("admin login 应返回 accessToken").isNotBlank();
        return token;
    }

    protected HttpHeaders adminHeaders(String token) {
        HttpHeaders h = new HttpHeaders();
        h.setBearerAuth(token);
        h.setContentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON);
        return h;
    }

    protected ResponseEntity<R> adminGet(String path, HttpHeaders h) {
        return rest.exchange(path, HttpMethod.GET, new HttpEntity<>(h), R.class);
    }

    protected ResponseEntity<R> adminPost(String path, String body, HttpHeaders h) {
        return rest.exchange(path, HttpMethod.POST, new HttpEntity<>(body, h), R.class);
    }

    protected ResponseEntity<R> adminPut(String path, String body, HttpHeaders h) {
        return rest.exchange(path, HttpMethod.PUT, new HttpEntity<>(body, h), R.class);
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    protected Map<String, Object> dataOf(ResponseEntity<R> resp) {
        assertThat(resp.getBody()).as("响应体不应为空").isNotNull();
        return (Map<String, Object>) resp.getBody().getData();
    }

    // ==================================================================
    //  request body helpers
    // ==================================================================

    /**
     * 构造 {@code POST /api/v1/orders} 的 body.
     *
     * <p>重量 100kg / 件数 10 件 / 服务项金额 1000 —— 供计费 IT 验算。
     */
    protected String createOrderBody() {
        return "{"
                + "\"customerId\":" + customerId + ","
                + "\"businessType\":\"OCEAN_FCL\","
                + "\"tradeDirection\":\"EXPORT\","
                + "\"incoterms\":\"FOB\","
                + "\"serviceType\":\"D2D\","
                + "\"originAddress\":\"上海洋山港\","
                + "\"destAddress\":\"洛杉矶港\","
                + "\"cargoReadyDate\":\"2026-11-20\","
                + "\"etd\":\"2026-11-25\","
                + "\"eta\":\"2026-12-10\","
                + "\"containerQty\":1,"
                + "\"containerTypeCode\":\"40HQ\","
                + "\"marksAndNos\":\"MSKU1234567\","
                + "\"cargoDescription\":\"五金配件\","
                + "\"isOversized\":false,"
                + "\"settlementCurrency\":\"CNY\","
                + "\"remark\":\"4.6 IT fixture\","
                + "\"cargo\":[{"
                + "\"cargoNameCn\":\"钢材\",\"cargoNameEn\":\"STEEL\",\"hsCode\":\"7208\","
                + "\"pkgType\":\"CARTON\",\"pkgQty\":10,\"unit\":\"CTN\","
                + "\"weightKg\":100,\"netWeightKg\":90,\"volumeCbm\":12.5,"
                + "\"lengthCm\":100,\"widthCm\":80,\"heightCm\":50,\"isStackable\":true,"
                + "\"cargoValue\":5000,\"currencyCode\":\"CNY\",\"isDangerous\":false"
                + "}],"
                + "\"serviceItems\":[{"
                + "\"feeType\":\"OCEAN_FREIGHT\",\"feeName\":\"海运费\",\"feeCategory\":\"FREIGHT\","
                + "\"calcBasis\":\"PER_CONTAINER\",\"qty\":1,\"unitPrice\":1000,\"amount\":1000,"
                + "\"currencyCode\":\"CNY\",\"isMandatory\":true"
                + "}]"
                + "}";
    }

    /** 构造带 reason 的转换端点 body。 */
    protected String reasonBody(String reason) {
        return "{\"reason\":\"" + reason + "\"}";
    }

    /** 从 create 响应里取新建订单的 id。 */
    protected Long orderIdOf(ResponseEntity<R> resp) {
        assertThat(resp.getStatusCode().value()).as("建单应 200, 实际 %d", resp.getStatusCode().value())
                .isEqualTo(200);
        Map<String, Object> data = dataOf(resp);
        Object id = data.get("id");
        assertThat(id).as("建单响应应含 id").isNotNull();
        long orderId = ((Number) id).longValue();
        trackOrder(orderId);
        return orderId;
    }

    /** 库里当前的 order_status。 */
    protected String statusOf(long orderId) {
        return jdbc.queryForObject(
                "SELECT order_status FROM t_order WHERE id = ?", String.class, orderId);
    }

    /** 该订单发出的 outbox 事件数 (按 aggregate_type='ORDER' + aggregate_id 隔离)。 */
    protected int outboxCount(long orderId) {
        Integer n = jdbc.queryForObject(
                "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM t_event_outbox WHERE aggregate_type = 'ORDER' AND aggregate_id = ?",
                Integer.class, String.valueOf(orderId));
        return n == null ? 0 : n;
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 2: `OrderLifecycleIT` —— 9 态全链路**

Create `lumen-parent/lumen-bootstrap/src/test/java/com/lumen/integration/OrderLifecycleIT.java`：

```java
package com.lumen.integration;

import com.lumen.common.api.R;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

/**
 * 4.6 — 货运委托单 9 态全链路 IT.
 *
 * <p>建单 → 提交 → 确认 → 开始操作 → 交付 → 结算 → 关账, 7 步连续走通,
 * 每一步断言 {@code t_order.order_status}, 最后断言 outbox 事件数。
 *
 * <p>每步都发一条 {@code OrderStateChangedEvent} (共 7 条) —— 建单**不发**
 * (DRAFT 是初始态, 不是一次「状态转换」)。
 */
@DisplayName("OrderLifecycle IT")
class OrderLifecycleIT extends OrderAdminFixtureBase {

    @Test
    @DisplayName("DRAFT → PENDING_CONFIRM → CONFIRMED → IN_OPERATION → DELIVERED → SETTLED → CLOSED")
    void fullLifecycle_walksThroughSevenTransitions() {
        String token = loginAdmin();
        HttpHeaders h = adminHeaders(token);

        // 建单 → DRAFT
        Long id = orderIdOf(adminPost("/api/v1/orders", createOrderBody(), h));
        assertThat(statusOf(id)).isEqualTo("DRAFT");
        assertThat(outboxCount(id)).as("建单不发状态变更事件").isZero();

        // DRAFT → PENDING_CONFIRM
        ResponseEntity<R> submit = adminPost("/api/v1/orders/" + id + "/submit", null, h);
        assertThat(submit.getStatusCode().value()).isEqualTo(200);
        assertThat(statusOf(id)).isEqualTo("PENDING_CONFIRM");

        // PENDING_CONFIRM → CONFIRMED
        ResponseEntity<R> confirm = adminPost("/api/v1/orders/" + id + "/confirm", null, h);
        assertThat(confirm.getStatusCode().value()).isEqualTo(200);
        assertThat(statusOf(id)).isEqualTo("CONFIRMED");

        // CONFIRMED → IN_OPERATION
        ResponseEntity<R> start = adminPost("/api/v1/orders/" + id + "/start-operation", null, h);
        assertThat(start.getStatusCode().value()).isEqualTo(200);
        assertThat(statusOf(id)).isEqualTo("IN_OPERATION");

        // IN_OPERATION → DELIVERED
        ResponseEntity<R> deliver = adminPost("/api/v1/orders/" + id + "/deliver", null, h);
        assertThat(deliver.getStatusCode().value()).isEqualTo(200);
        assertThat(statusOf(id)).isEqualTo("DELIVERED");

        // DELIVERED → SETTLED
        ResponseEntity<R> settle = adminPost("/api/v1/orders/" + id + "/settle", null, h);
        assertThat(settle.getStatusCode().value()).isEqualTo(200);
        assertThat(statusOf(id)).isEqualTo("SETTLED");

        // SETTLED → CLOSED
        ResponseEntity<R> close = adminPost("/api/v1/orders/" + id + "/close",
                reasonBody("4.6 IT 关账"), h);
        assertThat(close.getStatusCode().value()).isEqualTo(200);
        assertThat(statusOf(id)).isEqualTo("CLOSED");

        // 7 次转换 = 7 条 outbox 事件
        assertThat(outboxCount(id)).isEqualTo(7);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("列表按 status 过滤 + 详情返回 allowedNextStates")
    void list_filtersByStatus_andDetailReturnsAllowedNextStates() {
        String token = loginAdmin();
        HttpHeaders h = adminHeaders(token);
        Long id = orderIdOf(adminPost("/api/v1/orders", createOrderBody(), h));
        adminPost("/api/v1/orders/" + id + "/submit", null, h);

        ResponseEntity<R> list = adminGet("/api/v1/orders?current=1&size=10&status=PENDING_CONFIRM", h);
        assertThat(list.getStatusCode().value()).isEqualTo(200);
        assertThat(dataOf(list).get("records")).asInstanceOf(
                org.assertj.core.api.InstanceOfAssertFactories.LIST)
                .anyMatch(m -> String.valueOf(((Map<?, ?>) m).get("id")).equals(String.valueOf(id)));

        ResponseEntity<R> detail = adminGet("/api/v1/orders/" + id, h);
        assertThat(detail.getStatusCode().value()).isEqualTo(200);
        Map<String, Object> data = dataOf(detail);
        assertThat(data.get("orderStatus")).isEqualTo("PENDING_CONFIRM");
        assertThat((List<?>) data.get("allowedNextStates")).contains("CONFIRMED");
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: `OrderStateMachineSeedIT` —— seed 落地 + 非法转换 409**

Create `lumen-parent/lumen-bootstrap/src/test/java/com/lumen/integration/OrderStateMachineSeedIT.java`：

```java
package com.lumen.integration;

import com.lumen.common.api.R;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

/**
 * 4.6 — ORDER 状态机 seed 落地 + 非法转换 IT.
 *
 * <p>三件事:
 * <ol>
 *   <li>{@code t_state_transition} 里 {@code state_machine_code='ORDER'} 恰好 15 行,
 *       且 {@code tenant_id} 全是 0 (全局行 —— 填 1 会让其它租户看不到转换规则)。</li>
 *   <li>DRAFT 的出边恰好 3 条 (PENDING_CONFIRM / CANCELLED / VOID)。</li>
 *   <li>非法转换 (DRAFT → CONFIRMED) 返回 <b>409 + code 409_101</b>,
 *       <b>不是</b> StateMachineException 的 14001/400。</li>
 * </ol>
 */
@DisplayName("OrderStateMachineSeed IT")
class OrderStateMachineSeedIT extends OrderAdminFixtureBase {

    @Test
    @DisplayName("t_state_transition: ORDER 恰好 15 行, tenant_id 全是 0")
    void seed_hasFifteenGlobalRows() {
        Integer cnt = jdbc.queryForObject(
                "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM t_state_transition WHERE state_machine_code = 'ORDER'", Integer.class);
        assertThat(cnt).isEqualTo(15);

        Integer nonGlobal = jdbc.queryForObject(
                "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM t_state_transition WHERE state_machine_code = 'ORDER' AND tenant_id <> 0",
                Integer.class);
        assertThat(nonGlobal).as("状态机 seed 必须是 tenant_id=0 的全局行").isZero();
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("DRAFT 的出边恰好 3 条")
    void draft_hasThreeOutgoingTransitions() {
        List<String> toStates = jdbc.queryForList(
                "SELECT to_state FROM t_state_transition WHERE state_machine_code = 'ORDER' AND from_state = 'DRAFT'",
                String.class);
        assertThat(toStates).containsExactlyInAnyOrder("PENDING_CONFIRM", "CANCELLED", "VOID");
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("DRAFT → CONFIRMED 非法: 409 + code 409_101 (不是 400)")
    void illegalTransition_returns409_101() {
        String token = loginAdmin();
        HttpHeaders h = adminHeaders(token);
        Long id = orderIdOf(adminPost("/api/v1/orders", createOrderBody(), h));

        // DRAFT 直接 confirm —— 状态机不允许
        ResponseEntity<R> resp = adminPost("/api/v1/orders/" + id + "/confirm", null, h);

        assertThat(resp.getStatusCode().value())
                .as("非法转换应 409 (StateMachineException 是 400, 已被 service 转成 409)")
                .isEqualTo(409);
        assertThat(resp.getBody()).isNotNull();
        assertThat(resp.getBody().getCode()).isEqualTo(409_101);
        assertThat(statusOf(id)).isEqualTo("DRAFT");
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `OrderNumberingIT` —— 编号口径**

Create `lumen-parent/lumen-bootstrap/src/test/java/com/lumen/integration/OrderNumberingIT.java`：

```java
package com.lumen.integration;

import com.lumen.common.api.R;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;

import java.util.Map;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

/**
 * 4.6 — 委托单编号口径 IT.
 *
 * <p>编号由 {@code sys_number_rule} 的 code='ORDER' 产出 (V4 seed:
 * prefix='ORD', date_format='yyyyMMdd', seq_length=6, reset_policy='DAY')。
 * 口径 = {@code ORD-yyyyMMdd-6位流水}, 例如 {@code ORD-20261110-000001}。
 *
 * <p>本 IT 锁 3 件事: 格式、唯一、库里落的就是这个号 (不是前端拼的)。
 */
@DisplayName("OrderNumbering IT")
class OrderNumberingIT extends OrderAdminFixtureBase {

    @Test
    @DisplayName("order_no 形如 ORD-yyyyMMdd-6位流水, 且两单不同")
    void orderNo_followsFormatAndIsUnique() {
        String token = loginAdmin();
        HttpHeaders h = adminHeaders(token);

        Long first = orderIdOf(adminPost("/api/v1/orders", createOrderBody(), h));
        Long second = orderIdOf(adminPost("/api/v1/orders", createOrderBody(), h));

        String no1 = jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT order_no FROM t_order WHERE id = ?", String.class, first);
        String no2 = jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT order_no FROM t_order WHERE id = ?", String.class, second);

        assertThat(no1).matches("ORD-\\d{8}-\\d{6}");
        assertThat(no2).matches("ORD-\\d{8}-\\d{6}");
        assertThat(no1).isNotEqualTo(no2);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("建单响应里的 orderNo 与库里一致")
    void createResponse_orderNoMatchesDatabase() {
        String token = loginAdmin();
        HttpHeaders h = adminHeaders(token);

        ResponseEntity<R> resp = adminPost("/api/v1/orders", createOrderBody(), h);
        Long id = orderIdOf(resp);
        Map<String, Object> data = dataOf(resp);

        String inDb = jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT order_no FROM t_order WHERE id = ?", String.class, id);
        assertThat(data.get("orderNo")).isEqualTo(inDb);
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: `OrderBillingAccrualIT` —— 交付即计费**

Create `lumen-parent/lumen-bootstrap/src/test/java/com/lumen/integration/OrderBillingAccrualIT.java`：

```java
package com.lumen.integration;

import com.lumen.common.api.R;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;

import java.time.Duration;
import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;
import static org.awaitility.Awaitility.await;

/**
 * 4.6 — 委托单交付即计费 IT.
 *
 * <p>链路: 委托单走到 DELIVERED → 发 {@code OrderStateChangedEvent} →
 * finance 的 {@code BusinessEventListener} 反射读到 {@code toState=DELIVERED} →
 * 调 {@code BillingEngineService.accrue} → 写 {@code t_billing_accrual}
 * (business_type='ORDER', business_id=委托单 id)。
 *
 * <p><b>listener 是 {@code @Async("outboxExecutor")}</b> —— 所以断言必须 await,
 * 不能同步查完就断言 (那样会偶发红)。
 */
@DisplayName("OrderBillingAccrual IT")
class OrderBillingAccrualIT extends OrderAdminFixtureBase {

    @Test
    @DisplayName("DELIVERED → t_billing_accrual 出现 business_type=ORDER 的 OPEN 提单")
    void deliver_opensAccrual() {
        // business_type='ORDER' 的 ACTIVE 规则 —— 没有它 accrue 会走 markNoRule, 不写 accrual
        seedActiveOrderRule("weight * 2");

        String token = loginAdmin();
        HttpHeaders h = adminHeaders(token);
        Long id = orderIdOf(adminPost("/api/v1/orders", createOrderBody(), h));

        adminPost("/api/v1/orders/" + id + "/submit", null, h);
        adminPost("/api/v1/orders/" + id + "/confirm", null, h);
        adminPost("/api/v1/orders/" + id + "/start-operation", null, h);
        ResponseEntity<R> deliver = adminPost("/api/v1/orders/" + id + "/deliver", null, h);
        assertThat(deliver.getStatusCode().value()).isEqualTo(200);

        await().atMost(Duration.ofSeconds(15)).pollInterval(Duration.ofMillis(200)).untilAsserted(() -> {
            List<Map<String, Object>> rows = jdbc.queryForList(
                    "SELECT id, business_type, business_id, gross_amount, status, counterparty_id "
                        + "FROM t_billing_accrual WHERE business_type = 'ORDER' AND business_id = ?",
                    id);
            assertThat(rows).as("交付后应产生一条 ORDER 提单").hasSize(1);
            Map<String, Object> row = rows.get(0);
            assertThat(row.get("status")).isEqualTo("OPEN");
            // 规则表达式 "weight * 2", 货物重量 100kg → gross 200
            assertThat(((Number) row.get("gross_amount")).doubleValue()).isEqualTo(200.0);
            assertThat(((Number) row.get("counterparty_id")).longValue()).isEqualTo(customerId);
        });
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("未到 DELIVERED (CONFIRMED) 不产生提单")
    void notDelivered_doesNotOpenAccrual() {
        seedActiveOrderRule("weight * 2");

        String token = loginAdmin();
        HttpHeaders h = adminHeaders(token);
        Long id = orderIdOf(adminPost("/api/v1/orders", createOrderBody(), h));

        adminPost("/api/v1/orders/" + id + "/submit", null, h);
        adminPost("/api/v1/orders/" + id + "/confirm", null, h);

        // 给异步 listener 一点时间跑完 (确保它确实跑了, 而不是还没轮到)
        await().atMost(Duration.ofSeconds(15)).pollInterval(Duration.ofMillis(200)).untilAsserted(() -> {
            Integer n = jdbc.queryForObject(
                    "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM t_billing_accrual WHERE business_type = 'ORDER' AND business_id = ?",
                    Integer.class, id);
            assertThat(n).isZero();
        });
    }
}
```

> **`await().untilAsserted()` 的用法**：断言失败会**重试**直到超时, 所以
> 「未到 DELIVERED 不产生提单」这条用例要**先 sleep 一小段**再断言 0, 否则异步
> listener 还没跑到, 断言 0 会立刻通过 (= 假绿, 没真正验到「listener 跑过但没提单」）。
> 上面那条用例的写法是**反的** —— 它会立刻通过。**改成下面这个写法**：
>
> ```java
>         // 等 listener 真的跑过 (outbox 行被消费) 再断言没有提单
>         await().atMost(Duration.ofSeconds(15)).pollInterval(Duration.ofMillis(200)).until(() -> {
>             Integer dispatched = jdbc.queryForObject(
>                     "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM t_event_outbox WHERE aggregate_type = 'ORDER' AND aggregate_id = ?",
>                     Integer.class, String.valueOf(id));
>             return dispatched != null && dispatched >= 2;
>         });
>         Integer n = jdbc.queryForObject(
>                 "SELECT COUNT(*) FROM t_billing_accrual WHERE business_type = 'ORDER' AND business_id = ?",
>                 Integer.class, id);
>         assertThat(n).isZero();
> ```
>
> **实现时按这个版本写，不要按上面那个版本写。**

- [ ] **Step 6: `PortalFreightOrderIT` —— 门户 5 端点 + 跨客户 404**

Create `lumen-parent/lumen-bootstrap/src/test/java/com/lumen/integration/PortalFreightOrderIT.java`：

```java
package com.lumen.integration;

import com.lumen.common.api.R;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.AfterEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.BeforeEach;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.DisplayName;
import org.junit.jupiter.api.Test;
import org.springframework.beans.factory.annotation.Autowired;
import org.springframework.http.HttpEntity;
import org.springframework.http.HttpHeaders;
import org.springframework.http.HttpMethod;
import org.springframework.http.MediaType;
import org.springframework.http.ResponseEntity;
import org.springframework.jdbc.core.JdbcTemplate;
import org.springframework.security.crypto.bcrypt.BCryptPasswordEncoder;

import java.util.List;
import java.util.Map;
import java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicLong;

import static org.assertj.core.api.Assertions.assertThat;

/**
 * 4.6 — 门户端货运委托单 IT.
 *
 * <p>5 个端点全走一遍: 建单 → 我的列表 → 详情 → 确认 → 取消,
 * 外加两条越权防线: 跨客户访问 404 / 空 reason 400。
 *
 * <p><b>不直接复用 {@link PortalOrderFixtureBase}</b> —— 那个基类是为了城配配送单
 * (wms_delivery_order) 设计的, 本 IT 用的是 t_order, 表和清理逻辑都不同。
 *
 * <p><b>租户</b>: 门户 JWT 的 tid=1, 但仍带 {@code X-Tenant-Id: 1}
 * (与 {@link PortalOrderFixtureBase} 一致, 少一个变量少一个坑)。
 */
@DisplayName("PortalFreightOrder IT")
class PortalFreightOrderIT extends IntegrationBase {

    /** Portal 客户角色 id = 14 (V33 seed)。 */
    private static final long CUSTOMER_ROLE_ID = 14L;
    private static final String ALICE = "alice_order_46";
    private static final String ALICE_PWD = "alice-order46-pwd";
    private static final String CAROL = "carol_order_46";
    private static final String CAROL_PWD = "carol-order46-pwd";

    private static final AtomicLong FIXTURE_ID = new AtomicLong(971_001L);

    @Autowired JdbcTemplate jdbc;
    @Autowired BCryptPasswordEncoder passwordEncoder;

    private long aliceId;
    private long carolId;
    private long customerAliceId;
    private long customerCarolId;
    private String aliceToken;
    private String carolToken;

    @BeforeEach
    void seedPortal() {
        aliceId = nextId();
        seedUser(aliceId, ALICE, ALICE_PWD);
        bindRole(aliceId);
        customerAliceId = nextId();
        seedCustomer(customerAliceId, "C046-ALICE", "4.6 门户 Alice 客户");

        carolId = nextId();
        seedUser(carolId, CAROL, CAROL_PWD);
        bindRole(carolId);
        customerCarolId = nextId();
        seedCustomer(customerCarolId, "C046-CAROL", "4.6 门户 Carol 客户");

        aliceToken = login(ALICE, ALICE_PWD);
        carolToken = login(CAROL, CAROL_PWD);
    }

    @AfterEach
    void cleanPortal() {
        // 每条语句**独立**兜底 —— 一条失败不能连累后面 7 条。
        // (旧写法把 8 条塞进同一个 try: 第 1 条一抛异常, 后面 7 条全被 catch 吞掉, 数据残留)
        //
        // t_order_cargo **没有 customer_id 列** —— 货物明细只认 order_id,
        // 所以必须走 t_order 的子查询, 不能照抄 t_order 的过滤条件。
        // 子查询也带 tenant_id —— 外层带了内层不带, 过滤口径自相矛盾。
        runQuietly("DELETE FROM t_order_cargo WHERE tenant_id = 1 AND order_id IN "
                + "(SELECT id FROM t_order WHERE tenant_id = 1 AND customer_id IN (?, ?))",
                customerAliceId, customerCarolId);
        runQuietly("DELETE FROM t_order_service_item WHERE tenant_id = 1 AND order_id IN "
                + "(SELECT id FROM t_order WHERE tenant_id = 1 AND customer_id IN (?, ?))",
                customerAliceId, customerCarolId);
        // outbox 按 aggregate_id 精确删 —— 不能写成 "aggregate_type='ORDER'" 一把梭:
        // 那会连带删掉本租户其它用例产生的 ORDER 事件, 且 t_event_outbox.tenant_id 允许 NULL
        // (跨租户事件留 NULL), 带 tenant_id=1 会漏删。
        runQuietly("DELETE FROM t_event_outbox WHERE aggregate_type = 'ORDER' AND aggregate_id IN "
                + "(SELECT CAST(id AS CHAR) FROM t_order WHERE tenant_id = 1 AND customer_id IN (?, ?))",
                customerAliceId, customerCarolId);
        runQuietly("DELETE FROM t_order WHERE tenant_id = 1 AND customer_id IN (?, ?)",
                customerAliceId, customerCarolId);
        runQuietly("DELETE FROM t_customer_user_link WHERE tenant_id = 1 AND user_id IN (?, ?)",
                aliceId, carolId);
        runQuietly("DELETE FROM sys_user_role WHERE tenant_id = 1 AND user_id IN (?, ?)", aliceId, carolId);
        runQuietly("DELETE FROM sys_user WHERE tenant_id = 1 AND id IN (?, ?)", aliceId, carolId);
        runQuietly("DELETE FROM wms_customer WHERE tenant_id = 1 AND id IN (?, ?)",
                customerAliceId, customerCarolId);
    }

    /** teardown 里的单条 SQL —— 失败只记日志, 不让测试因为清理失败而红。 */
    private void runQuietly(String sql, Object... args) {
        try {
            jdbc.update(sql, args);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            System.out.println("[teardown] 清理失败(已忽略): " + e.getMessage());
        }
    }

    // ================================================================
    //  用例
    // ================================================================

    @Test
    @DisplayName("建单 → 我的列表 → 详情: 归属正确")
    void createListDetail_works() {
        HttpHeaders h = portalHeaders(aliceToken);

        ResponseEntity<R> create = postJson("/api/customer/freight-order", createBody(), h);
        assertThat(create.getStatusCode().value()).isEqualTo(200);
        Map<String, Object> created = dataOf(create);
        Long id = ((Number) created.get("id")).longValue();
        assertThat(created.get("orderStatus")).isEqualTo("DRAFT");
        assertThat(created.get("customerId")).isEqualTo((int) customerAliceId);

        // 我的列表 —— 只应有自己建的单
        ResponseEntity<R> list = getJson("/api/customer/freight-order?current=1&size=20", h);
        assertThat(list.getStatusCode().value()).isEqualTo(200);
        List<Map<String, Object>> records = (List<Map<String, Object>>) dataOf(list).get("records");
        assertThat(records).hasSize(1);
        assertThat(((Number) records.get(0).get("id")).longValue()).isEqualTo(id);

        // 详情
        ResponseEntity<R> detail = getJson("/api/customer/freight-order/" + id, h);
        assertThat(detail.getStatusCode().value()).isEqualTo(200);
        Map<String, Object> d = dataOf(detail);
        assertThat(d.get("orderNo")).isEqualTo(created.get("orderNo"));
        assertThat((List<?>) d.get("cargo")).hasSize(1);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("跨客户访问详情 → 404 (不泄露存在性)")
    void crossCustomerDetail_returns404() {
        HttpHeaders h = portalHeaders(aliceToken);
        ResponseEntity<R> create = postJson("/api/customer/freight-order", createBody(), h);
        Long id = ((Number) dataOf(create).get("id")).longValue();

        // Carol 访问 Alice 的单 —— 必须 404, 不是 403
        ResponseEntity<R> resp = getJson("/api/customer/freight-order/" + id, portalHeaders(carolToken));
        assertThat(resp.getStatusCode().value()).isEqualTo(404);
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("客户确认: PENDING_CONFIRM → CONFIRMED")
    void confirm_movesToConfirmed() {
        Long id = seedOrder(customerAliceId, "PENDING_CONFIRM");

        ResponseEntity<R> resp = postJson("/api/customer/freight-order/" + id + "/confirm", null,
                portalHeaders(aliceToken));

        assertThat(resp.getStatusCode().value()).isEqualTo(200);
        assertThat(statusOf(id)).isEqualTo("CONFIRMED");
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("客户取消: PENDING_CONFIRM → CANCELLED")
    void cancel_movesToCancelled() {
        Long id = seedOrder(customerAliceId, "PENDING_CONFIRM");

        ResponseEntity<R> resp = postJson("/api/customer/freight-order/" + id + "/cancel",
                "{\"reason\":\"客户临时取消这批货\"}", portalHeaders(aliceToken));

        assertThat(resp.getStatusCode().value()).isEqualTo(200);
        assertThat(statusOf(id)).isEqualTo("CANCELLED");
    }

    @Test
    @DisplayName("取消原因为空 → 400 (code 400_103)")
    void cancel_blankReason_returns400() {
        Long id = seedOrder(customerAliceId, "PENDING_CONFIRM");

        ResponseEntity<R> resp = postJson("/api/customer/freight-order/" + id + "/cancel",
                "{\"reason\":\"  \"}", portalHeaders(aliceToken));

        assertThat(resp.getStatusCode().value()).isEqualTo(400);
        assertThat(resp.getBody()).isNotNull();
        assertThat(resp.getBody().getCode()).isEqualTo(400_103);
    }

    // ================================================================
    //  helpers
    // ================================================================

    private long nextId() {
        return FIXTURE_ID.incrementAndGet();
    }

    private void seedUser(long id, String username, String rawPassword) {
        jdbc.update(
            "INSERT INTO sys_user (id, tenant_id, username, password_hash, real_name, status, "
                + "version, deleted, created_at, updated_at) "
                + "VALUES (?, 1, ?, ?, '4.6 portal', 1, 0, 0, NOW(), NOW())",
            id, username, passwordEncoder.encode(rawPassword), username);
    }

    private void bindRole(long userId) {
        long pk = Math.abs(System.nanoTime()) % 2_000_000_000L;
        try {
            jdbc.update(
                "INSERT INTO sys_user_role (id, user_id, role_id, tenant_id, created_at) "
                    + "VALUES (?, ?, ?, 1, NOW())", pk, userId, CUSTOMER_ROLE_ID);
        } catch (Exception dup) {
            // already bound
        }
    }

    private void seedCustomer(long id, String code, String name) {
        jdbc.update(
            "INSERT INTO wms_customer (id, tenant_id, code, name, deleted, version, created_at, updated_at) "
                + "VALUES (?, 1, ?, ?, 0, 1, NOW(), NOW())", id, code, name);
    }

    /** 预置一条指定状态的委托单 (含 1 条货物 + 1 条服务项)。 */
    private Long seedOrder(long customerId, String status) {
        long id = nextId();
        jdbc.update(
            "INSERT INTO t_order (id, tenant_id, order_no, business_type, customer_id, trade_direction, "
                + " incoterms, service_type, order_status, settlement_currency, total_qty, total_weight_kg, "
                + " total_volume_cbm, is_dangerous, is_oversized, created_at, updated_at, deleted, version) "
                + "VALUES (?, 1, ?, 'OCEAN_FCL', ?, 'EXPORT', 'FOB', 'D2D', ?, 'CNY', 10, 100, 12.5, "
                + " 0, 0, NOW(), NOW(), 0, 1)",
            id, "ORD-IT-" + id, customerId, status);
        jdbc.update(
            "INSERT INTO t_order_cargo (id, tenant_id, order_id, cargo_name_cn, pkg_type, pkg_qty, unit, "
                + " weight_kg, volume_cbm, is_stackable, is_dangerous, created_at, updated_at, deleted, version) "
                + "VALUES (?, 1, ?, '钢材', 'CARTON', 10, 'CTN', 100, 12.5, 1, 0, NOW(), NOW(), 0, 1)",
            nextId(), id);
        jdbc.update(
            "INSERT INTO t_order_service_item (id, tenant_id, order_id, fee_type, fee_name, fee_category, "
                + " calc_basis, qty, unit_price, amount, currency_code, is_mandatory, source, "
                + " created_at, updated_at, deleted, version) "
                + "VALUES (?, 1, ?, 'OCEAN_FREIGHT', '海运费', 'FREIGHT', 'PER_CONTAINER', 1, 1000, 1000, "
                + " 'CNY', 1, 'MANUAL', NOW(), NOW(), 0, 1)",
            nextId(), id);
        return id;
    }

    private String statusOf(long orderId) {
        return jdbc.queryForObject("SELECT order_status FROM t_order WHERE id = ?", String.class, orderId);
    }

    private String login(String username, String password) {
        HttpHeaders h = new HttpHeaders();
        h.setContentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON);
        h.set("X-Tenant-Id", "1");
        String body = "{\"username\":\"" + username + "\",\"password\":\"" + password + "\"}";
        ResponseEntity<R> resp = rest.exchange(
                "/api/auth/portal/login", HttpMethod.POST, new HttpEntity<>(body, h), R.class);
        assertThat(resp.getStatusCode().value()).as("portal login 应 200").isEqualTo(200);
        @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
        Map<String, Object> data = (Map<String, Object>) resp.getBody().getData();
        String token = (String) data.get("token");
        assertThat(token).isNotBlank();
        return token;
    }

    private HttpHeaders portalHeaders(String token) {
        HttpHeaders h = new HttpHeaders();
        h.setBearerAuth(token);
        h.set("X-Tenant-Id", "1");
        h.setContentType(MediaType.APPLICATION_JSON);
        return h;
    }

    private ResponseEntity<R> postJson(String path, String body, HttpHeaders h) {
        return rest.exchange(path, HttpMethod.POST, new HttpEntity<>(body, h), R.class);
    }

    private ResponseEntity<R> getJson(String path, HttpHeaders h) {
        return rest.exchange(path, HttpMethod.GET, new HttpEntity<>(h), R.class);
    }

    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
    private Map<String, Object> dataOf(ResponseEntity<R> resp) {
        assertThat(resp.getBody()).isNotNull();
        return (Map<String, Object>) resp.getBody().getData();
    }

    private String createBody() {
        return "{"
                + "\"businessType\":\"OCEAN_FCL\","
                + "\"tradeDirection\":\"EXPORT\","
                + "\"incoterms\":\"FOB\","
                + "\"serviceType\":\"D2D\","
                + "\"originAddress\":\"上海洋山港\","
                + "\"destAddress\":\"洛杉矶港\","
                + "\"settlementCurrency\":\"CNY\","
                + "\"remark\":\"4.6 门户 IT\","
                + "\"cargo\":[{\"cargoNameCn\":\"钢材\",\"pkgType\":\"CARTON\",\"pkgQty\":10,"
                + "\"unit\":\"CTN\",\"weightKg\":100,\"volumeCbm\":12.5}],"
                + "\"serviceItems\":[{\"feeType\":\"OCEAN_FREIGHT\",\"feeName\":\"海运费\","
                + "\"calcBasis\":\"PER_CONTAINER\",\"qty\":1,\"unitPrice\":1000,\"amount\":1000}]"
                + "}";
    }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: 建 service（13 个端点）**

Create `lumen-admin-web/src/services/order/orderService.ts`：

```ts
/**
 * Lumen Admin — 货运委托单 service (Iteration 4.6).
 *
 * 后端: `lumen-parent/lumen-order/src/main/java/com/lumen/order/controller/OrderController.java`
 *       路径前缀 `/api/v1/orders`, 13 个端点。
 *
 * <p><b>路径一律用相对形式 `/orders`</b> —— `services/request.ts` 的 axios baseURL
 * 已经是 `/api/v1`。
 *
 * <p><b>为什么不是 `/api/v1/orders`</b>: 仓里既有 ~30 个 service 写的是绝对
 * `/api/v1/xxx`, axios 会拼成 `/api/v1/api/v1/xxx`（已用
 * `axios.create({baseURL:'/api/v1'}).getUri({url:'/api/v1/x'})` 实测确认）。
 * 那是**既有缺陷**, 不在本轮修复范围 —— Task 11 会把它写进文档订正清单。
 * 新代码不复制这个 bug。
 */

import { request } from '@/services/request';
import type { ApiEnvelope, PageResult } from '@/services/request';

/**
 * `asData` 住在 `services/dispatch/_util.ts` (Iteration 4.1)。
 *
 * 它是**通用**的 axios 拦截器适配器 (1 行), 不是 dispatch 专属 ——
 * 这里复用而不是复制第三份。把它搬到 `services/_util.ts` 要改 30 个文件,
 * 超出本轮范围。
 */
import { asData } from '@/services/dispatch/_util';

/** 后端 `R<T>` 信封的本地别名。 */
type R<T> = ApiEnvelope<T>;

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Types — mirror backend dto/OrderView.java / OrderDetailView.java
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

/** 货物明细行 —— 对应 `CargoView`。 */
export interface OrderCargoItem {
  id: number;
  cargoNameCn?: string | null;
  cargoNameEn?: string | null;
  hsCode?: string | null;
  pkgType?: string | null;
  pkgQty?: number | null;
  unit?: string | null;
  weightKg?: number | null;
  netWeightKg?: number | null;
  volumeCbm?: number | null;
  lengthCm?: number | null;
  widthCm?: number | null;
  heightCm?: number | null;
  isStackable?: number | null;
  cargoValue?: number | null;
  currencyCode?: string | null;
  brandName?: string | null;
  model?: string | null;
  isDangerous?: number | null;
  unNo?: string | null;
  dangerClass?: string | null;
  remark?: string | null;
}

/** 服务项行 —— 对应 `ServiceItemView`。 */
export interface OrderServiceItem {
  id: number;
  feeType?: string | null;
  feeName?: string | null;
  feeCategory?: string | null;
  calcBasis?: string | null;
  qty?: number | null;
  unitPrice?: number | null;
  amount?: number | null;
  currencyCode?: string | null;
  supplierId?: number | null;
  costAmount?: number | null;
  isMandatory?: number | null;
  source?: string | null;
  remark?: string | null;
}

/** 列表行 —— 对应 `OrderView`。 */
export interface OrderRow {
  id: number;
  orderNo: string;
  customerId: number;
  businessType?: string | null;
  tradeDirection?: string | null;
  incoterms?: string | null;
  serviceType?: string | null;
  originAddress?: string | null;
  destAddress?: string | null;
  totalQty?: number | null;
  totalWeightKg?: number | null;
  totalVolumeCbm?: number | null;
  orderStatus: string;
  settlementCurrency?: string | null;
  etd?: string | null;
  eta?: string | null;
  createdAt?: string | null;
}

/** 详情 —— 对应 `OrderDetailView` (= OrderRow + 明细 + 允许的下一状态)。 */
export interface OrderDetail extends OrderRow {
  cargoReadyDate?: string | null;
  containerQty?: number | null;
  containerTypeCode?: string | null;
  marksAndNos?: string | null;
  cargoDescription?: string | null;
  isDangerous?: number | null;
  isOversized?: number | null;
  temperatureRange?: string | null;
  remark?: string | null;
  cancelReason?: string | null;
  closeReason?: string | null;
  voidReason?: string | null;
  submittedAt?: string | null;
  confirmedAt?: string | null;
  deliveredAt?: string | null;
  settledAt?: string | null;
  closedAt?: string | null;
  createdBy?: number | null;
  updatedBy?: number | null;
  cargo: OrderCargoItem[];
  serviceItems: OrderServiceItem[];
  /** 状态机允许的下一状态 —— 前端据此渲染动作按钮。 */
  allowedNextStates: string[];
}

/** 列表查询参数 —— 与后端 `OrderController.page` 的 query 参数一一对应。 */
export interface OrderPageQuery {
  current?: number;
  size?: number;
  status?: string;
  customerId?: number;
  orderNo?: string;
  businessType?: string;
}

/** 建单 / 编辑的 body —— 与后端 `CreateOrderRequest` 一一对应。 */
export interface CreateOrderPayload {
  customerId: number;
  businessType: string;
  tradeDirection: string;
  incoterms: string;
  serviceType: string;
  originAddress?: string | null;
  destAddress?: string | null;
  cargoReadyDate?: string | null;
  etd?: string | null;
  eta?: string | null;
  containerQty?: number | null;
  containerTypeCode?: string | null;
  marksAndNos?: string | null;
  cargoDescription?: string | null;
  isOversized?: boolean | null;
  temperatureRange?: string | null;
  settlementCurrency?: string | null;
  remark?: string | null;
  cargo: Array<Partial<OrderCargoItem>>;
  serviceItems: Array<Partial<OrderServiceItem>>;
}

// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
// Service
// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

export const orderService = {
  /** 1. 分页列表。 */
  async page(query: OrderPageQuery): Promise<PageResult<OrderRow>> {
    return asData<PageResult<OrderRow>>(
      request.get<R<PageResult<OrderRow>>>('/orders', { params: query }),
    );
  },

  /** 2. 详情。 */
  async get(id: number): Promise<OrderDetail> {
    return asData<OrderDetail>(request.get<R<OrderDetail>>(`/orders/${id}`));
  },

  /** 3. 建单 (落 DRAFT)。 */
  async create(payload: CreateOrderPayload): Promise<OrderRow> {
    return asData<OrderRow>(request.post<R<OrderRow>>('/orders', payload));
  },

  /** 4. 编辑 (仅 DRAFT / PENDING_CONFIRM)。 */
  async update(id: number, payload: CreateOrderPayload): Promise<OrderDetail> {
    return asData<OrderDetail>(request.put<R<OrderDetail>>(`/orders/${id}`, payload));
  },

  /** 5. 提交 (DRAFT → PENDING_CONFIRM)。 */
  async submit(id: number): Promise<OrderDetail> {
    return asData<OrderDetail>(request.post<R<OrderDetail>>(`/orders/${id}/submit`));
  },

  /** 6. 确认 (PENDING_CONFIRM → CONFIRMED)。 */
  async confirm(id: number): Promise<OrderDetail> {
    return asData<OrderDetail>(request.post<R<OrderDetail>>(`/orders/${id}/confirm`));
  },

  /** 7. 退回 (PENDING_CONFIRM → DRAFT), 必须带原因。 */
  async returnToDraft(id: number, reason: string): Promise<OrderDetail> {
    return asData<OrderDetail>(
      request.post<R<OrderDetail>>(`/orders/${id}/return`, { reason }),
    );
  },

  /** 8. 取消, 必须带原因。 */
  async cancel(id: number, reason: string): Promise<OrderDetail> {
    return asData<OrderDetail>(
      request.post<R<OrderDetail>>(`/orders/${id}/cancel`, { reason }),
    );
  },

  /** 9. 作废, 必须带原因。 */
  async voidOrder(id: number, reason: string): Promise<OrderDetail> {
    return asData<OrderDetail>(
      request.post<R<OrderDetail>>(`/orders/${id}/void`, { reason }),
    );
  },

  /** 10. 开始操作 (CONFIRMED → IN_OPERATION)。 */
  async startOperation(id: number): Promise<OrderDetail> {
    return asData<OrderDetail>(
      request.post<R<OrderDetail>>(`/orders/${id}/start-operation`),
    );
  },

  /** 11. 交付 (IN_OPERATION → DELIVERED)。 */
  async deliver(id: number): Promise<OrderDetail> {
    return asData<OrderDetail>(request.post<R<OrderDetail>>(`/orders/${id}/deliver`));
  },

  /** 12. 结算 (DELIVERED → SETTLED)。 */
  async settle(id: number): Promise<OrderDetail> {
    return asData<OrderDetail>(request.post<R<OrderDetail>>(`/orders/${id}/settle`));
  },

  /** 13. 关闭 (SETTLED → CLOSED), 必须带原因。 */
  async close(id: number, reason: string): Promise<OrderDetail> {
    return asData<OrderDetail>(
      request.post<R<OrderDetail>>(`/orders/${id}/close`, { reason }),
    );
  },
};
```

- [ ] **Step 2: 建状态标签组件（不复用通用 StatusTag）**

Create `lumen-admin-web/src/components/order/OrderStatusTag.tsx`：

```tsx
/**
 * 货运委托单状态标签 (Iteration 4.6).
 *
 * <p><b>不复用 `components/columns/StatusTag.tsx`</b> —— 那个通用组件里
 * `CLOSED` 已经映射成「已关闭」(配送异常工单), 而委托单的 `CLOSED` 是
 * 「已归档」; `DELIVERED` 在城配是「已签收」, 在委托单是「已交付」。
 * 语义不同, 复用会显示错标签。
 */

import { Tag } from 'antd';

interface Props {
  value?: string | null;
}

const COLORS: Record<string, string> = {
  DRAFT: 'default',
  PENDING_CONFIRM: 'gold',
  CONFIRMED: 'blue',
  IN_OPERATION: 'processing',
  DELIVERED: 'cyan',
  SETTLED: 'purple',
  CLOSED: 'default',
  CANCELLED: 'default',
  VOID: 'red',
};

const LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  DRAFT: '草稿',
  PENDING_CONFIRM: '待确认',
  CONFIRMED: '已确认',
  IN_OPERATION: '操作中',
  DELIVERED: '已交付',
  SETTLED: '已结算',
  CLOSED: '已归档',
  CANCELLED: '已取消',
  VOID: '已作废',
};

export function OrderStatusTag({ value }: Props) {
  const key = (value ?? '').toUpperCase();
  return <Tag color={COLORS[key] ?? 'default'}>{LABELS[key] ?? key ?? '未知'}</Tag>;
}

export default OrderStatusTag;
```

```tsx
/**
 * Lumen Admin — 货运委托单列表 (Iteration 4.6).
 *
 * 筛选项: 委托单号 / 状态 / 业务类型 / 客户 ID。点击行进入详情。
 */

import type { ActionType, ProColumns } from '@ant-design/pro-components';
import { ProTable } from '@ant-design/pro-components';
import { App as AntApp, Button } from 'antd';
import { useRef } from 'react';
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router-dom';

import { DateTimeColumn } from '@/components/columns/DateTimeColumn';
import { OrderStatusTag } from '@/components/order/OrderStatusTag';
import { useAccess } from '@/hooks/useAccess';
import { orderService } from '@/services/order/orderService';
import type { OrderRow } from '@/services/order/orderService';

const STATUS_OPTIONS = [
  { value: 'DRAFT', label: '草稿' },
  { value: 'PENDING_CONFIRM', label: '待确认' },
  { value: 'CONFIRMED', label: '已确认' },
  { value: 'IN_OPERATION', label: '操作中' },
  { value: 'DELIVERED', label: '已交付' },
  { value: 'SETTLED', label: '已结算' },
  { value: 'CLOSED', label: '已归档' },
  { value: 'CANCELLED', label: '已取消' },
  { value: 'VOID', label: '已作废' },
];

const BUSINESS_TYPE_OPTIONS = [
  { value: 'OCEAN_FCL', label: '海运整箱' },
  { value: 'OCEAN_LCL', label: '海运拼箱' },
  { value: 'AIR', label: '空运' },
  { value: 'RAIL', label: '铁路' },
  { value: 'TRUCK', label: '陆运' },
];

export default function OrderList() {
  const actionRef = useRef<ActionType>();
  const nav = useNavigate();
  const { message: antMessage } = AntApp.useApp();
  const access = useAccess();

  const columns: ProColumns<OrderRow>[] = [
    { title: '委托单号', dataIndex: 'orderNo', width: 180 },
    { title: '客户 ID', dataIndex: 'customerId', width: 100, hideInSearch: true },
    {
      title: '业务类型',
      dataIndex: 'businessType',
      width: 120,
      valueType: 'select',
      fieldProps: { options: BUSINESS_TYPE_OPTIONS },
    },
    { title: '贸易方向', dataIndex: 'tradeDirection', width: 100, hideInSearch: true },
    { title: '贸易条款', dataIndex: 'incoterms', width: 100, hideInSearch: true },
    { title: '服务类型', dataIndex: 'serviceType', width: 100, hideInSearch: true },
    { title: '起运地', dataIndex: 'originAddress', width: 160, ellipsis: true },
    { title: '目的地', dataIndex: 'destAddress', width: 160, ellipsis: true },
    {
      title: '重量 (kg)',
      dataIndex: 'totalWeightKg',
      width: 100,
      hideInSearch: true,
      render: (_, r) => r.totalWeightKg ?? '—',
    },
    {
      title: '体积 (m³)',
      dataIndex: 'totalVolumeCbm',
      width: 100,
      hideInSearch: true,
      render: (_, r) => r.totalVolumeCbm ?? '—',
    },
    {
      title: '状态',
      dataIndex: 'orderStatus',
      width: 110,
      valueType: 'select',
      fieldProps: { options: STATUS_OPTIONS },
      render: (_, r) => <OrderStatusTag value={r.orderStatus} />,
    },
    {
      title: 'ETD',
      dataIndex: 'etd',
      width: 110,
      hideInSearch: true,
    },
    {
      title: 'ETA',
      dataIndex: 'eta',
      width: 110,
      hideInSearch: true,
    },
    {
      title: '创建时间',
      dataIndex: 'createdAt',
      width: 170,
      hideInSearch: true,
      render: (_, r) => <DateTimeColumn value={r.createdAt} />,
    },
    {
      title: '操作',
      valueType: 'option',
      width: 140,
      render: (_, record) => [
        <Button
          key="detail"
          type="link"
          size="small"
          disabled={!access.canRead('order:view')}
          onClick={() => nav(`/orders/${record.id}`)}
        >
          详情
        </Button>,
      ],
    },
  ];

  return (
    <ProTable<OrderRow>
      headerTitle="货运委托单"
      actionRef={actionRef}
      rowKey="id"
      columns={columns}
      search={{ labelWidth: 'auto' }}
      request={async (params) => {
        try {
          const res = await orderService.page({
            current: params.current,
            size: params.pageSize,
            orderNo: params.orderNo as string | undefined,
            status: params.orderStatus as string | undefined,
            businessType: params.businessType as string | undefined,
            customerId: params.customerId as number | undefined,
          });
          return { data: res.records, total: res.total, success: true };
        } catch {
          antMessage.error('委托单列表加载失败');
          return { data: [], total: 0, success: false };
        }
      }}
      pagination={{ defaultPageSize: 20, showSizeChanger: true }}
      toolBarRender={() => [
        <Button
          key="add"
          type="primary"
          disabled={!access.canRead('order:create')}
          onClick={() => nav('/orders/new')}
        >
          新建委托单
        </Button>,
      ]    />
  );
}
```

> **`DateTimeColumn` 的入参**是 `string | null | undefined` —— 传 `r.createdAt` 即可。

- [ ] **Step 4: 详情页（动作按钮由 `allowedNextStates` 驱动）**

Create `lumen-admin-web/src/pages/Order/Detail.tsx`：

```tsx
/**
 * Lumen Admin — 货运委托单详情 (Iteration 4.6).
 *
 * <p><b>动作按钮由后端返回的 `allowedNextStates` 驱动</b> —— 前端不自己判断
 * 「当前状态能不能做什么」, 而是让状态机 (`t_state_transition`) 单点决定。
 * 9 态状态机里每个目标状态的入边唯一, 所以「目标状态 → 动作」是一一对应的。
 */

import {
  App as AntApp,
  Button,
  Card,
  Col,
  Descriptions,
  Input,
  Modal,
  Row,
  Spin,
  Table,
  Tag,
} from 'antd';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';

import { OrderStatusTag } from '@/components/order/OrderStatusTag';
import { useAccess } from '@/hooks/useAccess';
import { orderService } from '@/services/order/orderService';
import type {
  OrderCargoItem,
  OrderDetail,
  OrderServiceItem,
} from '@/services/order/orderService';

const { TextArea } = Input;

/** 目标状态 → 动作。9 态状态机里每个目标状态的入边唯一, 所以这个映射是无歧义的。 */
const ACTIONS: Array<{
  to: string;
  label: string;
  perm: string;
  needReason?: boolean;
  run: (id: number, reason?: string) => Promise<OrderDetail>;
}> = [
  { to: 'PENDING_CONFIRM', label: '提交', perm: 'order:submit', run: (id) => orderService.submit(id) },
  { to: 'CONFIRMED', label: '确认', perm: 'order:confirm', run: (id) => orderService.confirm(id) },
  { to: 'DRAFT', label: '退回', perm: 'order:submit', needReason: true, run: (id, r) => orderService.returnToDraft(id, r!) },
  { to: 'IN_OPERATION', label: '开始操作', perm: 'order:operate', run: (id) => orderService.startOperation(id) },
  { to: 'DELIVERED', label: '交付', perm: 'order:operate', run: (id) => orderService.deliver(id) },
  { to: 'SETTLED', label: '结算', perm: 'order:settle', run: (id) => orderService.settle(id) },
  { to: 'CLOSED', label: '关闭归档', perm: 'order:close', needReason: true, run: (id, r) => orderService.close(id, r!) },
  { to: 'CANCELLED', label: '取消', perm: 'order:cancel', needReason: true, run: (id, r) => orderService.cancel(id, r!) },
  { to: 'VOID', label: '作废', perm: 'order:void', needReason: true, run: (id, r) => orderService.voidOrder(id, r!) },
];

/** 原因最少 5 个字 —— 与后端 `OrderConstants.REASON_MIN_LENGTH` 一致。 */
const REASON_MIN = 5;

export default function OrderDetailPage() {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
  const orderId = Number(id);
  const nav = useNavigate();
  const { message: antMessage } = AntApp.useApp();
  const access = useAccess();

  const [detail, setDetail] = useState<OrderDetail | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [pending, setPending] = useState<{ label: string; needReason?: boolean; run: (id: number, reason?: string) => Promise<OrderDetail> } | null>(null);
  const [reason, setReason] = useState('');
  const [acting, setActing] = useState(false);

  const load = async () => {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      setDetail(await orderService.get(orderId));
    } catch {
      antMessage.error('委托单详情加载失败');
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    void load();
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [orderId]);

  const submitReason = async () => {
    if (!pending) return;
    if (pending.needReason && reason.trim().length < REASON_MIN) {
      antMessage.error(`原因至少 ${REASON_MIN} 个字`);
      return;
    }
    setActing(true);
    try {
      await pending.run(orderId, reason.trim());
      antMessage.success(`${pending.label}成功`);
      setPending(null);
      setReason('');
      await load();
    } catch {
      // 后端错误已由 request.ts 的全局通知弹出, 这里不重复提示。
    } finally {
      setActing(false);
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return <Spin />;
  }
  if (!detail) {
    return <Card>委托单不存在</Card>;
  }

  const actions = ACTIONS.filter(
    (a) => detail.allowedNextStates.includes(a.to) && access.canRead(a.perm),
  );

  return (
    <Card
      title={
        <span>
          {detail.orderNo} <OrderStatusTag value={detail.orderStatus} />
        </span>
      }
      extra={
        <span>
          {actions.map((a) => (
            <Button
              key={a.to}
              type={a.to === 'VOID' ? 'default' : 'primary'}
              danger={a.to === 'VOID'}
              style={{ marginLeft: 8 }}
              onClick={() => {
                setPending(a);
                setReason('');
              }}
            >
              {a.label}
            </Button>
          ))}
          <Button style={{ marginLeft: 8 }} onClick={() => nav('/orders')}>
            返回列表
          </Button>
        </span>
      }
    >
      <Descriptions bordered column={3} size="small">
        <Descriptions.Item label="客户 ID">{detail.customerId}</Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="业务类型">{detail.businessType ?? '—'}</Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="贸易方向">{detail.tradeDirection ?? '—'}</Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="贸易条款">{detail.incoterms ?? '—'}</Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="服务类型">{detail.serviceType ?? '—'}</Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="结算币种">{detail.settlementCurrency ?? '—'}</Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="起运地">{detail.originAddress ?? '—'}</Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="目的地">{detail.destAddress ?? '—'}</Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="ETD / ETA">{`${detail.etd ?? '—'} / ${detail.eta ?? '—'}`}</Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="重量 (kg)">{detail.totalWeightKg ?? '—'}</Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="体积 (m³)">{detail.totalVolumeCbm ?? '—'}</Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="件数">{detail.totalQty ?? '—'}</Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="箱量">{detail.containerQty ?? '—'}</Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="箱型">{detail.containerTypeCode ?? '—'}</Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="唛头">{detail.marksAndNos ?? '—'}</Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="货物描述" span={3}>
          {detail.cargoDescription ?? '—'}
        </Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="超限">
          {detail.isOversized ? <Tag color="orange">是</Tag> : '否'}
        </Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="温区">{detail.temperatureRange ?? '—'}</Descriptions.Item>
        <Descriptions.Item label="备注">{detail.remark ?? '—'}</Descriptions.Item>
      </Descriptions>

      <Card size="small" title="货物明细" style={{ marginTop: 16 }}>
        <Table<OrderCargoItem>
          rowKey="id"
          size="small"
          pagination={false}
          dataSource={detail.cargo}
          columns={[
            { title: '品名 (中)', dataIndex: 'cargoNameCn' },
            { title: '品名 (英)', dataIndex: 'cargoNameEn' },
            { title: 'HS 编码', dataIndex: 'hsCode' },
            { title: '件数', dataIndex: 'pkgQty' },
            { title: '重量 (kg)', dataIndex: 'weightKg' },
            { title: '体积 (m³)', dataIndex: 'volumeCbm' },
            { title: '品牌', dataIndex: 'brandName' },
          ]}
        />
      </Card>

      <Card size="small" title="服务项 / 费用" style={{ marginTop: 16 }}>
        <Table<OrderServiceItem>
          rowKey="id"
          size="small"
          pagination={false}
          dataSource={detail.serviceItems}
          columns={[
            { title: '费用类型', dataIndex: 'feeType' },
            { title: '费用名称', dataIndex: 'feeName' },
            { title: '计费基准', dataIndex: 'calcBasis' },
            { title: '数量', dataIndex: 'qty' },
            { title: '单价', dataIndex: 'unitPrice' },
            { title: '金额', dataIndex: 'amount' },
            { title: '币种', dataIndex: 'currencyCode' },
            { title: '我方成本', dataIndex: 'costAmount' },
          ]}
        />
      </Card>

      <Modal
        open={pending != null}
        title={`${pending?.label ?? ''} — 请填写原因`}
        onCancel={() => setPending(null)}
        onOk={submitReason}
        confirmLoading={acting}
        okText="确认"
        cancelText="取消"
      >
        <TextArea
          rows={4}
          value={reason}
          maxLength={500}
          onChange={(e) => setReason(e.target.value)}
          placeholder={`至少 ${REASON_MIN} 个字`}
        />
      </Modal>
    </Card>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: 建单 / 编辑表单**

Create `lumen-admin-web/src/pages/Order/Form.tsx`：

```tsx
/**
 * Lumen Admin — 货运委托单建单 / 编辑表单 (Iteration 4.6).
 *
 * <p>有 `:id` 路由参数 → 编辑模式 (先 GET 详情回填, 提交走 PUT);
 * 无参数 → 新建模式 (提交走 POST, 建出来是 DRAFT)。
 */

import {
  App as AntApp,
  Button,
  Card,
  DatePicker,
  Form,
  Input,
  InputNumber,
  Select,
  Space,
  Spin,
} from 'antd';
import dayjs, { Dayjs } from 'dayjs';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { useNavigate, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';

import { orderService } from '@/services/order/orderService';
import type { CreateOrderPayload } from '@/services/order/orderService';

const { TextArea } = Input;

interface FormValues {
  customerId: number;
  businessType: string;
  tradeDirection: string;
  incoterms: string;
  serviceType: string;
  originAddress?: string;
  destAddress?: string;
  cargoReadyDate?: Dayjs;
  etd?: Dayjs;
  eta?: Dayjs;
  containerQty?: number;
  containerTypeCode?: string;
  marksAndNos?: string;
  cargoDescription?: string;
  isOversized?: boolean;
  temperatureRange?: string;
  settlementCurrency?: string;
  remark?: string;
  cargo?: Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
  serviceItems?: Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
}

const BUSINESS_TYPE_OPTIONS = [
  { value: 'OCEAN_FCL', label: '海运整箱' },
  { value: 'OCEAN_LCL', label: '海运拼箱' },
  { value: 'AIR', label: '空运' },
  { value: 'RAIL', label: '铁路' },
  { value: 'TRUCK', label: '陆运' },
];

const TRADE_DIRECTION_OPTIONS = [
  { value: 'IMPORT', label: '进口' },
  { value: 'EXPORT', label: '出口' },
];

const INCOTERM_OPTIONS = [
  { value: 'FOB', label: 'FOB' },
  { value: 'CIF', label: 'CIF' },
  { value: 'DAP', label: 'DAP' },
  { value: 'DDP', label: 'DDP' },
  { value: 'EXW', label: 'EXW' },
  { value: 'FCA', label: 'FCA' },
];

const SERVICE_TYPE_OPTIONS = [
  { value: 'D2D', label: '门到门' },
  { value: 'DDP', label: '完税交货' },
  { value: 'D2P', label: '门到港' },
];

export default function OrderForm() {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
  const editing = id != null;
  const orderId = editing ? Number(id) : null;
  const nav = useNavigate();
  const { message: antMessage } = AntApp.useApp();
  const [form] = Form.useForm<FormValues>();
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(editing);
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!editing || orderId == null) return;
    void (async () => {
      setLoading(true);
      try {
        const d = await orderService.get(orderId);
        form.setFieldsValue({
          customerId: d.customerId,
          businessType: d.businessType ?? undefined,
          tradeDirection: d.tradeDirection ?? undefined,
          incoterms: d.incoterms ?? undefined,
          serviceType: d.serviceType ?? undefined,
          originAddress: d.originAddress ?? undefined,
          destAddress: d.destAddress ?? undefined,
          cargoReadyDate: d.cargoReadyDate ? dayjs(d.cargoReadyDate) : undefined,
          etd: d.etd ? dayjs(d.etd) : undefined,
          eta: d.eta ? dayjs(d.eta) : undefined,
          containerQty: d.containerQty ?? undefined,
          containerTypeCode: d.containerTypeCode ?? undefined,
          marksAndNos: d.marksAndNos ?? undefined,
          cargoDescription: d.cargoDescription ?? undefined,
          isOversized: d.isOversized === 1,
          temperatureRange: d.temperatureRange ?? undefined,
          settlementCurrency: d.settlementCurrency ?? undefined,
          remark: d.remark ?? undefined,
          cargo: d.cargo.map((c) => ({ ...c })),
          serviceItems: d.serviceItems.map((s) => ({ ...s })),
        });
      } catch {
        antMessage.error('委托单详情加载失败');
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    })();
  }, [editing, orderId, form, antMessage]);

  const onSubmit = async () => {
    let values: FormValues;
    try {
      values = await form.validateFields();
    } catch {
      return; // antd 已就地提示
    }
    const payload: CreateOrderPayload = {
      customerId: values.customerId,
      businessType: values.businessType,
      tradeDirection: values.tradeDirection,
      incoterms: values.incoterms,
      serviceType: values.serviceType,
      originAddress: values.originAddress ?? null,
      destAddress: values.destAddress ?? null,
      cargoReadyDate: values.cargoReadyDate ? values.cargoReadyDate.format('YYYY-MM-DD') : null,
      etd: values.etd ? values.etd.format('YYYY-MM-DD') : null,
      eta: values.eta ? values.eta.format('YYYY-MM-DD') : null,
      containerQty: values.containerQty ?? null,
      containerTypeCode: values.containerTypeCode ?? null,
      marksAndNos: values.marksAndNos ?? null,
      cargoDescription: values.cargoDescription ?? null,
      isOversized: values.isOversized ?? null,
      temperatureRange: values.temperatureRange ?? null,
      settlementCurrency: values.settlementCurrency ?? null,
      remark: values.remark ?? null,
      cargo: (values.cargo ?? []).map((c) => {
        const { id: _id, ...rest } = c as Record<string, unknown> & { id?: number };
        void _id;
        return rest;
      }),
      serviceItems: (values.serviceItems ?? []).map((s) => {
        const { id: _id, ...rest } = s as Record<string, unknown> & { id?: number };
        void _id;
        return rest;
      }),
    };
    setSubmitting(true);
    try {
      if (editing && orderId != null) {
        await orderService.update(orderId, payload);
        antMessage.success('委托单已保存');
        nav(`/orders/${orderId}`);
      } else {
        const created = await orderService.create(payload);
        antMessage.success('委托单已创建');
        nav(`/orders/${created.id}`);
      }
    } catch {
      // request.ts 已弹通知。
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  };

  if (loading) {
    return <Spin />;
  }

  return (
    <Card title={editing ? '编辑委托单' : '新建委托单'}>
      <Form<FormValues> form={form} layout="vertical">
        <Form.Item
          name="customerId"
          label="客户 ID"
          rules={[{ required: true, message: '请填写客户 ID' }]}
        >
          <InputNumber style={{ width: 220 }} min={1} />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="businessType" label="业务类型" rules={[{ required: true }]}>
          <Select options={BUSINESS_TYPE_OPTIONS} />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="tradeDirection" label="贸易方向" rules={[{ required: true }]}>
          <Select options={TRADE_DIRECTION_OPTIONS} />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="incoterms" label="贸易条款" rules={[{ required: true }]}>
          <Select options={INCOTERM_OPTIONS} />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="serviceType" label="服务类型" rules={[{ required: true }]}>
          <Select options={SERVICE_TYPE_OPTIONS} />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="originAddress" label="起运地">
          <Input maxLength={255} />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="destAddress" label="目的地">
          <Input maxLength={255} />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="cargoReadyDate" label="货好日期">
          <DatePicker />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="etd" label="ETD">
          <DatePicker />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="eta" label="ETA">
          <DatePicker />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="containerQty" label="箱量">
          <InputNumber min={0} />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="containerTypeCode" label="箱型">
          <Input maxLength={16} />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="marksAndNos" label="唛头">
          <Input maxLength={500} />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="cargoDescription" label="货物描述">
          <TextArea rows={2} maxLength={500} />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="isOversized" label="超限">
          <Select
            allowClear
            options={[
              { value: true, label: '是' },
              { value: false, label: '否' },
            ]}
          />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="temperatureRange" label="温区">
          <Input maxLength={32} />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="settlementCurrency" label="结算币种">
          <Input maxLength={8} placeholder="CNY" />
        </Form.Item>
        <Form.Item name="remark" label="备注">
          <TextArea rows={3} maxLength={1000} />
        </Form.Item>

        <Form.List name="cargo">
          {(fields, { add, remove }) => (
            <Card size="small" title="货物明细" style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
              {fields.map((field) => (
                <Space key={field.key} wrap align="start">
                  <Form.Item
                    {...field}
                    name={[field.name, 'cargoNameCn']}
                    rules={[{ required: true, message: '请填写品名' }]}
                  >
                    <Input placeholder="品名 (中)" style={{ width: 160 }} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item {...field} name={[field.name, 'pkgQty']}>
                    <InputNumber placeholder="件数" min={0} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item {...field} name={[field.name, 'weightKg']}>
                    <InputNumber placeholder="重量 kg" min={0} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item {...field} name={[field.name, 'volumeCbm']}>
                    <InputNumber placeholder="体积 m³" min={0} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item {...field} name={[field.name, 'hsCode']}>
                    <Input placeholder="HS 编码" style={{ width: 140 }} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Button danger onClick={() => remove(field.name)}>
                    删除
                  </Button>
                </Space>
              ))}
              <Button onClick={() => add({})}>添加货物明细</Button>
            </Card>
          )}
        </Form.List>

        <Form.List name="serviceItems">
          {(fields, { add, remove }) => (
            <Card size="small" title="服务项 / 费用" style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
              {fields.map((field) => (
                <Space key={field.key} wrap align="start">
                  <Form.Item
                    {...field}
                    name={[field.name, 'feeName']}
                    rules={[{ required: true, message: '请填写费用名称' }]}
                  >
                    <Input placeholder="费用名称" style={{ width: 160 }} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item {...field} name={[field.name, 'feeType']}>
                    <Input placeholder="费用类型" style={{ width: 120 }} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item {...field} name={[field.name, 'qty']}>
                    <InputNumber placeholder="数量" min={0} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item {...field} name={[field.name, 'unitPrice']}>
                    <InputNumber placeholder="单价" min={0} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item {...field} name={[field.name, 'amount']}>
                    <InputNumber placeholder="金额" min={0} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item {...field} name={[field.name, 'currencyCode']}>
                    <Input placeholder="币种" style={{ width: 80 }} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Form.Item {...field} name={[field.name, 'costAmount']}>
                    <InputNumber placeholder="我方成本" min={0} />
                  </Form.Item>
                  <Button danger onClick={() => remove(field.name)}>
                    删除
                  </Button>
                </Space>
              ))}
              <Button onClick={() => add({})}>添加服务项</Button>
            </Card>
          )}
        </Form.List>

        <Space>
          <Button type="primary" loading={submitting} onClick={onSubmit}>
            保存
          </Button>
          <Button onClick={() => nav('/orders')}>取消</Button>
        </Space>
      </Form>
    </Card>
  );
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: 加路由**

改 `lumen-admin-web/src/App.tsx`：

1. 在 `const DispatchDashboard = lazy(...)` 那行**后面**加：

```tsx
const OrderList = lazy(() => import('@/pages/Order/List'));
const OrderDetail = lazy(() => import('@/pages/Order/Detail'));
const OrderForm = lazy(() => import('@/pages/Order/Form'));
```

2. 在 `<Route path="/dispatch/dashboard" ...>` 那条**后面**加三条路由：

```tsx
                        <Route
                          path="/orders"
                          element={<Suspense fallback={pageFallback}><OrderList /></Suspense>}
                        />
                        <Route
                          path="/orders/new"
                          element={<Suspense fallback={pageFallback}><OrderForm /></Suspense>}
                        />
                        <Route
                          path="/orders/:id"
                          element={<Suspense fallback={pageFallback}><OrderDetail /></Suspense>}
                        />
                        <Route
                          path="/orders/:id/edit"
                          element={<Suspense fallback={pageFallback}><OrderForm /></Suspense>}
                        />
```

> **路由顺序**: React Router v6 按路由表顺序匹配, 但静态段优先于动态段 ——
> `/orders/new` **不会**被 `/orders/:id` 抢走 (`new` 是静态段)。顺序仍按上表写,
> 读起来更清楚。
